市場焦點
聯準會9月
升息一碼
• 聯邦基準利率升至 3.75%~4.00%，經濟預測部分上調2026-2028年GDP成長率、失業率皆下
調，通膨降溫進程仍相對緩慢 ，點陣圖顯示今年還有升息一碼的機會 ，這次升息對股票而言 ，
聯準會沒有準備以大幅犧牲經濟與就業換取通膨下降 ，降低了硬著陸風險；對債券而言 ，短
端仍須消化更高的政策利率，但長端反而可能受惠於抗通膨可信度改善。
AI模型
開發放緩
• Anthropic、OpenAI及xAI相繼支持放緩最先進AI模型開發速度。不過，前沿模型開發速度放
緩，並不代表AI基礎建設需求同步轉弱。晶片、算力、資料中心及能源等AI基礎建設仍處於供
不應求格局 ，短期若因市場情緒或資金槓桿調整而出現回檔 ，較可能反映估值與持倉修正 ，

而非AI需求基本面反轉 。後續若前期累積的槓桿壓力逐步消化 ，且全球總體經濟及地緣政治
風險未進一步惡化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值。
台灣央行
• 利率決議重點： (1)台灣央行宣布基準利率維持不變 ，重貼現率、擔保放款融通利率和短期融
通利率分別維持2%、2.375%和4.25%。(2)放鬆房市信用管制 ，調整自然人第2戶購屋貸款最
高成數上限，由6成調升為7成。(3)上修今年經濟成長率及通膨率 ，GDP上修至11.48%、CPI
及核心CPI分別上修至2.03%及2.16。(4)央行預估明年AI基礎建設需求將持續貢獻台灣出口表
現，不過由於基期已墊高，預測明年台灣經濟成長率為5.82%。

資料來源：Fed，華爾街見聞，2026.9.17
貨幣決議與9月、7月聲明稿差異，不再強調能源衝擊 全員支持升息
• 聯準會9月升息一碼，聯邦基準利率在3.75%~4.00%。
聲明稿重點差異
紅色字體為9月的新增部分，括號內藍色字體為刪除的7月聲明措辭
FOMC以12票贊成升息一碼 批准發布以下聲明 ：委員會決定 ，將聯邦基金利率目標 區間上調至 3.75%至4%維持在
3.50%至3.75%，以支持聯準會的雙重使命。委員會延續其維持銀行體系充足準備金的政策。
儘管中東衝突在一定程度上帶來了高水準的不確定性 ，美國經濟活動持續以穩健步調擴張 。儘管地緣政治情勢 等因素
使經濟前景仍籠 罩高度不確定性，但國內支出保持韌性，生產力成長強勁，企業資本投資也相當強勁穩健。新增就業

人數大致跟上勞動力成長速度，失業率也變化不大，顯示就業市場目前仍大致維持穩定。
相較於委員會2%的目標，通貨膨脹率依然偏高 ，這在一定程度上反映了部分特定部門 (包括能源)因供給衝擊所導致的
價格上漲。今日的政策行動，將有助於讓通膨更及時地回到2%目標，委員會將實現價格穩定。

聯準會會後記者會問答重點整理
1
通膨「過高且持續太久」 當
前重心偏向物價穩定
通膨數據未改善，通膨過高且持續太久，聯準會當前的政策重心偏向雙重使命中的物價穩定 。透過政策
行動表明，聯準會對抑制通膨是認真的。
2
經濟、就業與地緣政治促成
一致升息
近幾週公布的數據證實美國經濟正在走強 ，基礎經濟成長率高於先前判斷；其次 ，勞動市場依然穩健；
第三，通膨持續高於目標，地緣政治局勢也改變經濟展望 。華許表示，這三項因素共同促成當天「堅定
且一致」的決定。
3
點狀圖暗示今年再升 1碼 但
不是政策承諾
最新經濟預測顯示，多數官員認為今年仍有進一步升息空間。12 名官員預期年底前再升息 1 碼，另有 4
人預期還會升息 2 碼，僅 2 人主張利率維持在目前水準。不過，點狀圖反映的是官員對適當利率路徑的

個別預測，並非正式的承諾。
4
拒絕提供前瞻指引 不預判下
次會議結果
面對下一次升息可能落在 10 月或 12 月的追問，華許表示「我不給出前瞻指引。」
他強調，先前公開表達的是對貨幣政策紀律的承諾 ，而不是對特定利率決策的承諾 。聯準會不會預判未
來任何會議結果，後續行動仍將依據最新經濟數據及展望作出決定。
5
升息非市場逼迫 迴避川普施
壓問題
面對此次升息是否由債市波動及市場壓力所推動的提問，華許予以否認。
他表示是根據就業走勢、經濟強度及整體情勢作出決定 ，市場價格可作為觀察依據，但不能決定聯準會
的政策。
資料來源：Fed，華爾街見聞，2026.9.17

市場反應與投資建議
9/16
市場反應
• 市場將華許記者會解讀為偏鷹，那斯達克指數、標普
500指數、道瓊工業指數皆下跌。
• 10 年 期 美 債 殖 利 率 在 利 率 決 議 公 布 後 一 度 降 至
4.9323%，隨後回升至接近 4.99%，在關鍵的5%關
口附近波動。
• 2年期殖利率則由盤中低點4.5975%升至4.7124%，
反映投資人重新提高對後續升息的預期。
• 黃金跌破4,260美元。
• 利率市場顯示 ，交易員加大 聯準會在2026 年底前進
一步升息的押注，12月升息機率升至約50%。
投資建議
• 股市：AI長期趨勢不變，但需留意利率對評價面的影
響，逢拉回就分批買進，相對看好美、台、韓相關供
應鏈
• 債市：預期美債利率 維持高位震盪格局 ，目前債市

配置仍以鎖利為主，且聚焦中短天期。
資料來源：芝商所，2026.9.17

台灣利率連10凍 明年經濟展望佳
• 利率決議重點： (1)台灣央行宣布基準利率維持不變 ，重貼現率、擔保放款融通利率和短期融通利率分別維持 2%、2.375%
和4.25%。(2)放鬆房市信用管制，調整自然人第2戶購屋貸款最高成數上限，由6成調升為7成。(3)上修今年經濟成長率及通
膨率，GDP上修至11.48%(六月預估9.45 %)、CPI及核心CPI分別上修至2.03%及2.16%(六月預估1.91%及1.90%)。
• 觀點：全球AI資本支出維持上調的趨勢，台股供應鏈訂單直接受惠，央行預估明年AI基礎建設需求將持續貢獻台灣出口表現，
不過由於基期已墊高，預測明年台灣經濟成長率為5.82%。
• 台股短線展望：上週隨著聯準會利率決議公布後，台股量能回升，顯示短線消息面或已利空出盡。權值股台積電領軍台灣加

權指數站上月線，技術指標偏正向。投資佈局方面，由於地緣政治、通膨及貿易關稅風險仍存在不確定性，建議AI概念持股
核心布局行業龍頭企業，留意中小型成長股較易受到外部因素而股價大幅波動。
資料來源：中央銀行，2026.9.17。f代表央行預測數。

美國零售強勁回升擴大內需支撐，惟價格壓力與升息提高市場波動
• 零售全面回升，消費動能展現廣度：美國8月零售銷售月增1.2%，創3月以來最大增幅，優於市場預期，並扭轉7月修正
後月減0.5%；非實體店面銷售月增 2.6%、電子及家電增 1.6%，餐飲服務亦增 1.2%，且扣除汽車與汽油後仍增 1.2%，
控制組更增1.4%，顯示消費回升並非僅由汽車與燃料支出帶動，第三季內需仍具支撐。
• 名目消費偏強，通膨與升息增添需求壓力： 不過8月零售銷售年增6.0%屬名目增幅，部分反映物價上升因素；同期 CPI
年增3.4%，汽油價格月增3.9%，能源成本上升可能壓縮家庭實質購買力 ，加上Fed於9月決議升息1碼，後續利率政策
仍將視通膨與經濟數據調整，使市場焦點由消費回升轉向評估高利率與高物價對後續需求動能的影響。

• 後市觀察：短期零售回升仍有助支撐美國景氣，但若能源成本持續走高，加上升息後借貸成本上升，消費動能能否維持
將成為重要觀察點；投資配置可偏向美國投資級短中天期債券 ，兼顧收益與利率風險，股票則可聚焦獲利穩定、具定價
能力的大型優質企業，降低需求放緩與資金成本升高的影響。
近三月美國零售銷售月率變化(%)

科技企業自由現金流可望由目前約2,450億增至2028年的6,330億美元，支撐亞洲科技供應鏈中長期發展。短期若因市場情

緒或資金槓桿調整而出現回檔，較可能反映估值與持倉修正，而非AI需求基本面反轉。後續若前期累積的槓桿壓力逐步消化，
且全球總體經濟及地緣政治風險未進一步惡化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值。

油價在美國中期選舉前，或將維持在高檔
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.14
伊朗戰爭期間卡達油氣收入暴跌
卡達政府的石油和天然氣收入 美伊衝突
• 美伊衝突短期內仍難見明確解方，彭博預估雙方後續可能採取的行動，主要可分為以下四種情境：
(1)伊朗承受經濟封鎖：伊朗承受美國經濟制裁與施壓，避免進一步重大軍事升級。在美方護航行動支持下，商船仍可維持通行
荷姆茲海峽，國際原油供應未出現明顯中斷，油價預估在每桶80美元區間波動。
(2)伊朗將攻擊範圍限於海灣地區油輪與美軍：伊朗主要鎖定海灣地區能源運輸船隻及美軍目標，美方則採取有限度軍事回應，

衝突維持在一定範圍內，油價預估在每桶90美元區間波動。
(3)伊朗擴大攻擊範圍：攻擊目標不再局限於海灣地區船隻及美軍基地，海灣能源基礎設施以及紅海等重要航運咽喉，均可能成
為目標。局勢升級期間，油價可能維持在每桶100美元以上，待衝突緩和後回落至70至80美元區間，此情境發生的可能性最高。
(4)美伊雙方全面擴大開戰：伊朗進一步擴大攻擊目標，導致美國軍事資產及區域能源基礎設施遭受重大破壞，甚至不排除以色
列重新加入戰局，油價可能升至每桶120美元。

全球債市拋售勢頭不墜，AI融資競逐資金推升美債殖利率
• 長端殖利率走高，財政與通膨壓力仍是主因 ：近期美國長
天期公債殖利率持續走高，其中10年期美債殖利率更突破
5%，創2007年以來高點；除能源價格上升推升通膨疑慮
外，美國財政赤字、政府債務及持續性的發債需求 ，也提
高市場對長天期公債供給與期限溢酬的要求 ，反映長端利
率壓力仍未完全紓解。
• AI企業擴大融資，增加公司債供給與資金競逐： AI資料中
心、晶片與電力基礎建設的資本支出快速增加 ，帶動大型
科技企業提高債券融資規模；截至 6月，華爾街市場策略
研究機構Yardeni Research在追蹤外國私人投資人過去一
年買進美國公司債約3,900億美元，高於美國公債約3,290
億美元，顯示資金配置確有轉向公司債的現象 ，不過市場

研究亦指出，AI企業發債對美債殖利率的直接推升效果相
對有限，應可視為推動長天期利率上行的眾多因素之一。
• 後市觀察：若財政融資與AI基礎建設支出持續維持高檔 ，
長天期公債仍面臨較大的供給與資金配置壓力；但 AI投資
仍具中長期成長動能 ，配置上可關注資料中心 、半導體及
電力基礎建設供應鏈 ，固定收益則以短中天期美債及高品
質投資級公司債為主，降低長天期利率波動的影響。

新興市場本幣債券擁高票息提供防禦力
• 高票息提供防禦力：對債券投資人而言，票息就像安全墊。當市場利率上升、債券價格下跌時，持續收到的票息可以部分甚
至完全抵銷價格損失。新興市場本幣政府債券今年的表現，比市場原先預期更具韌性。目前新興市場本幣債券的持有收益約
為4.54%，已經足以對利率風險形成相當程度的保護 ，即使今年存續期間對多數市場造成負面影響 ，票息收益仍讓整體報酬
維持正數。這也是為什麼新興市場本幣債券在美伊衝突爆發後一度重挫超過 6%，但之後仍能逐步反彈 。哥倫比亞、巴西、
匈牙利及墨西哥的債券表現尤其突出。
• 市場已反映相當程度的升息風險：問題在於未來幾個月投資環境可能比上半年更加困難，能源價格上漲推升通膨，也迫使新
興市場央行維持較為鷹派的貨幣政策 。市場目前甚至預期 ，19個主要新興市場中 ，有18個市場的債券殖利率可能在年底前

上升。殖利率上升意味著債券價格承壓，因此投資人不能只看表面的高票息，必須同時評估未來利率變化可能造成資本損失。
新興市場本幣債券各因子對報酬率貢獻
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.15
新興市場本幣債券票息的貢獻度

新興市場本幣債券目前最大風險在於利率與匯率
• 存續期間仍是今年最主要的拖累因素：一般而言，債券的存續期間越高，當殖利率上升時，債券價格下跌的幅度就可能越大。
今年19個符合基準指數條件的新興市場中 ，有14個市場的總報酬受到存續期間拖累 ，其中韓國、馬來西亞、印尼、泰國及
菲律賓受到的負面影響最為明顯。更值得注意的是，新興市場債券的利率表現與美國公債高度相關，因此，投資新興市場債
券，不能只觀察當地央行，也必須密切留意美國聯準會利率政策及美國公債殖利率走勢。
• 匯率可能成為下一個重要變數：新興市場本幣債券除了債券價格本身，還存在另一項重要風險：匯率。今年即期匯率變動反
而對新興市場本幣債券帶來正面貢獻 ，約增加57個基點。但如果能源價格持續上漲並形成滯脹環境 ，順周期貨幣可能面臨

更大的貶值壓力 。目前市場預期年底前新興市場貨幣整體仍偏弱 ，19種新興市場貨幣中 ，有11種預計兌美元貶值 。其中土
耳其、哥倫比亞及巴西貨幣的潛在跌幅相對較大。這代表即使債券本身提供不錯的票息，如果當地貨幣大幅貶值，換算成美
元或其他投資人本國貨幣後，仍可能侵蝕甚至吞噬債券收益。
• 小結與投資建議：目前新興市場本幣債券比較像是高票息換取較高波動的投資，而不是單純追求資本利得的交易。在殖利率
已處於相對高檔、但利率與匯率風險仍未完全解除的情況下，採取分散市場、控制部位、逢回布局的策略會比追高更為合適。

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.15

• 多重資產配置可有效風險管理：美伊衝突持續及聯準會利率政策，使今年金融市場震盪加劇，加上第四季美國期中選舉到來，
增加市場更多不確定性，不過從歷史經驗來看，地緣政治事件對股市的影響多屬短期現象，市場在初期情緒性下跌後，通常會
逐步回歸基本面，相較於預測事件發展，投資人更應透過多元資產配置分散風險，以提升投資組合的穩定性與長期抗震能力。
• 透過多重資產廣納收益來源：在利率維持相對高檔的環境下，投資人更應重視收益來源的多元化，從下圖可見，相較於過去幾
年，掩護性買權、非投資等級債，及投資等級債等資產的收益率均顯著提升，提供投資組合更具吸引力的現金流來源，其中掩
護性買權收益率已超過10%，非投資等級債，及投資等級債亦分別達7%及5%以上水準，透過結合不同收益型資產，不僅有助

提升整體收益潛力，更能降低對單一市場或利率變動的依賴，在市場波動加劇時提高投資組合的穩定度與韌性，兼顧收益與長
期成長目標。

7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。

Update
Accton Technology Corporation  | Asia Pacific
September Sales -1% MoM/
+66% YoY
In this report, we focus on Accton's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for share price movement.
Details:
• September sales were NT$40,399mn (-1% MoM/+66% YoY).
• 3Q26 revenue was NT$120,744mn (+26% QoQ/+66% YoY), in line with MSe
of NT$120,883mn (+27% QoQ/+66% YoY)  and consensus of NT$117.390mn
(+23% QoQ/+61% YoY) by 3%.
Our view:
• We believe the consignment arrangement of DRAM, PCB, etc., from August

might have capped the upside on revenue growth, but it should be a
favorable factor for gross margin, emerging from August/September and
likely extending into 4Q26.
• Driven by the network switch business and its major customer's AI
accelerator module ramp, we expect revenue to continue growing
sequentially in 4Q26, with the momentum in 2027 strengthened by potential
new customers and new products.
• Stay OW with a PT of NT$3,500, based on 30x 2027e P/E.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Accton Technology Corporation (2345.TW)
Base case, P/E, which we believe better captures the company's earnings growth momentum.
We assign a target P/E of 30x to our 2027 EPS estimate, which we view as justified by an
operating profit CAGR of 48% for 2025-28, based on our estimates. This implies a PEG of
around 0.6, vs. its average of 0.7 over 2019-25, when the stock was trading at a P/E of 22x for
an operating profit CAGR of 32%.

Risks to Upside
n Stronger data center capex
n Faster network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand losing share in AI networks
n Stronger custom AI accelerator growth
n Stronger ODM direct business model adoption
Risks to Downside
n Weaker data center capex
n Slower network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand taking more share in AI networks
n Softer custom AI accelerator growth
n Softer ODM direct business model adoption

STOCK RATINGS

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더할 모멘텀삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더_21961458.pdf

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
미국 록빌·5공장 가세로 3분기 기대치 부합 전망 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 연결 매출액은 1조 3,637억 원(+8.4% Y oY), 
영업이익은 6,092억 원(-3.8% YoY, 영업이익률 44.7%)으로 시장 기대치에 부합할 
전망이다. 5월 파업에 따른 생산 차질에도 1~4공장(총 60.4만 리터)의 풀가동과 
신규 공장의 매출 기여가 외형 성장을 뒷받침할 것으로 예상한다. 미국 록빌 
공장(6만 리터)은 3분기부터 매출 기여가 본격화되고, 5공장(18만 리터)도 
인증용(PPQ) 배치 생산을 통해 매출이 확대될 전망이다. 2분기에는 미국 공장·5공장 
관련 비용이 선반영됐으나, 3분기에는 매출 증가에 따른 고정비 부담 완화로 
영업이익률이 전분기 대비 0.3%p 상승할 것으로 예상한다. 
항체 중심 CDMO에서 펩타이드로 확장 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 신규 수주는 약 1.2조 원으로 전년 수주액의 약 20% 
수준에 그치고 있으나, 연말부터 내년까지 수주 모멘텀은 점차 강화될 것으로 
판단한다. 2025년 하반기 이후 미국의 의약품 관세 불확실성이 완화되며 글로벌 
제약사와의 논의가 늘어나고 있어, 신규 수주 계약으로 이어질 것으로 기대된다. 
11월 유상증자 납입이 마무리되면 6공장(18만 리터) 착공과 스위스 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 본격화되며 중장기 성장 기대감도 높아질 전망이다. 특히 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 연내 완료되면 2027년부터 연결 실적에 반영되는 동시에, 항체 의약품 
중심의 CDMO 사업을 GLP-1 등 펩타이드로 확장하는 계기가 될 전망이다. 
목표주가 200만 원으로 하향, 수주 회복이 관건 
삼성바이오로직스의 목표주가를 209만 원에서 200만 원으로 하향한다. 유상증자 
신주 발행에 따른 주당가치 4.7% 희석 효과를 반영했다. 폴리펩타이드 인수 효과는 
거래완료 이후 실적 추정치에 반영할 예정이다. 노조 파업 불확실성이 주가에 
부담으로 작용할 수 있어 신규 수주 모멘텀의 회복이 필요한 시점이다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

Document 143: [402340] SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대하이닉스 자사주 소각_31fbe439

Source PDF: all report pdf/[402340] SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대하이닉스 자사주 소각_31fbe439.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

SK 스퀘어 (402340/KS) 
하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대 
예정보다 빠른 SK 하이닉스 자사주 매입 
SK스퀘어의 자회사인 SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 20일부터 11월 19일까지 자사주 40
조원(2,407만주, 3.3%)을 매입 예정이다. 9월 29일까지 1,715만주, 예정 매입수량 
대비 71.3%를 매입 완료하였다. 매입 초기 매일 65만주 매입하다가 9월 10일부터는 
60만주를 매입하고 있는데, 이러한 추세가 유지된다면 10월 중순경 자사주 매입이 마
무리될 예정이다. 자사주 매입 후 소각 예정으로 소각이 완료되면 SK스퀘어의 SK하아
닉스에 대한 지분율은 20.0%에서 20.7%로 상승하게 된다.

자사주 매입 이후 추가적인 주주환원에 대한 기대 
SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 19일 정책기간(‘25년~’27년) 동안 발생하는 누적 FCF의 
50% 이상을 주주환원 하는 주주환원 정책을 발표한 바 있다. 26년 SK하이닉스의 예
상 FCF는 164조원(컨센서스 기준)으로 50%를 주주환원 할 경우 자사주 매입 금액
(40 조원)을 제외한 주주환원 재원은 42 조원이다. 잔여 주주환원 재원이 모두 배당으
로 지급된다면 SK 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후 SK 스퀘어의 지분율은 20.7%이므로 
SK스퀘어의 배당수입도 8.7조원에 달한다. 지난 3월 SK스퀘어는 ‘26년~’28년 경
상배당수입의 30% 이상을 주주에게 환원하는 정책을 발표했다. 보수적으로 30%만 
가정하더라도 SK스퀘어의 배당재원은 2.6조원(현 시가총액 대비 1.8%)이다. SK스퀘
어는 연초 주주총회에서 배당가능이익 확보 차원에서 자본준비금 5 조 8,900억원을 
이익잉여금으로 전입한 바 있다. 이는 26년 재무제표가 확정되는 27년 3월 이후 배당
가능이익으로 편입되므로 SK스퀘어의 주주환원 확대가 기대된다.

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 185 만원(유지) 
SK스퀘어의 순자산 가치 중 98% 이상이 SK하이닉스 지분가치이다. SK하이닉스는 
자사주 매입 이후 17.7% 주가가 상승했으나, SK 스퀘어 주가는 11.4% 상승했다. SK
하이닉스 자사주 매입·소각은 SK 스퀘어의 보유 지분가치 상승뿐 아니라 지분율도 상
승하기 때문에 자사주가 소각될 경우 SK스퀘어의 주가가 탄력적으로 움직일 가능성이 
높다. SK스퀘어 현재 NAV 대비 할인율은 43.7%, 12MF PBR 1.4배로 7월 이후 주
가 조정으로 밸류에이션 매력도 높아졌다. 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 185만원을 유지
한다.

2026-09-30

지주회사/통신서비스/ESG. 최관순 / ks1.choi@sks.co.kr / 3773-8812

Document 154: AI帮储户多赚钱，银行却慌了：花旗、美银、摩根大通集体对AI Agent设防

Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-17

### Page 1

花旗、美银与摩根⼤通相继限制 AI 代理访问客⼾账⼾并代⾏交易，主因系担忧 AI ⼯具⾃动
将资⾦转⾄⾼息产品，威胁数万亿美元低成本存款。⽬前交易责任认定仍属空⽩，美联储
监管层私下对此表⽰⽀持。银⾏业正通过技术设防与提升存款黏性，应对 AI 引发的利润挑
战。
摘要：
花旗、美国银⾏和摩根⼤通相继宣布限制 AI 智能代理访问客⼾账⼾，这场集体设防的背后，是银
⾏业对数万亿美元低成本存款可能被 AI" 扫⾛ " 的深层焦虑。
花旗明确表⽰，不允许 " 第三⽅ AI 代理或助⼿登录客⼾账⼾，或直接在其数字银⾏平台上进⾏交
易 " ，称此举旨在保障客⼾账⼾与信息安全。美国银⾏⾸席执⾏官 Brian Moynihan 本周三在接受
CNBC 采访时表⽰，该⾏正在限制 AI 代理访问消费者存款账⼾，同时研究在 AI 发起⽀付指令时如
何界定责任主体。摩根⼤通⾸席执⾏官 Jamie Dimon 则在该⾏三季度业绩电话会议上表⽰，将限
制 AI 代理访问客⼾账⼾，理由是 " 你不会希望出现流氓代理，⽽且这类代理可能数以千计 " 。

与此同时，华尔街⾼管已就消费者使⽤ AI 代理进⾏⾦融交易的快速增⻓，与美联储监管⼈员展开
沟通；监管层私下对银⾏封堵 AI 代理访问客⼾账⼾的举措持欢迎态度。
存款流失威胁利润根基
此次银⾏业集体⾏动的直接导⽕索，是 AI 个⼈理财⼯具的加速涌现。据英国《⾦融时报》报道，
Meta 于 9 ⽉推出 Muse AI 个⼈助⼿后，业界担忧情绪明显升温 —— 市场普遍担⼼ AI 代理将⾃动替
消费者将闲置资⾦从银⾏账⼾转移⾄收益更⾼的产品。
阿波罗⾸席经济学家 Torsten Slok 在⼀份报告中将这⼀⻛险量化： AI 助⼿可能⾃动将家庭闲置资
⾦从全国平均仅 0.1% 的活期账⼾利率，转移⾄利率达 3.3% ⾄ 5% 的账⼾。报告警告称， " 如果每
个家庭都使⽤ AI 代理优化现⾦余额收益，银⾏可能损失⼤量⽤于发放贷款的低成本存款，这将对
整个⾦融体系构成威胁。 "
摩根⼤通、美国银⾏和富国银⾏⽬前合计持有 1.6 万亿美元的⽆息存款，占其负债总额的 16% 。
若被迫对这部分存款⽀付 3% 的利率，三家银⾏每年将额外承担约 470 亿美元的成本 —— 相当于

其年度税后净利润总和的近⼀半。
各⾏表态与责任认定困境
在设防⽅式上，各⾏有所差异，但核⼼逻辑⼀致：在责任归属尚不明确之前，不允许 AI 代理⾃主
操作账⼾资⾦。
Brian Moynihan 明确表⽰， " 我们强烈认为，账⼾转账授权应有⼈⼯参与。 " 他表⽰，美国银⾏正
与 AI 代理服务商沟通，提⽰其注意消费者可能事后对资⾦去向产⽣异议的⻛险。⼀位知情⼈⼠证
实，美国银⾏已限制 AI 代理的相关访问权限。
Jamie Dimon 则着重强调⻛险管控逻辑：
" 我们必须以正确的⽅式保护客⼾。我们正在构建相应⼯具。在此之前，我们不会允许任何外部
代理进⼊公司系统，因为这对客⼾和摩根⼤通⽽⾔⻛险太⼤。 "

上 AI 代理的演进速度，将成为决定这场博弈⾛向的两⼤关键变量。

Document 199: Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-05)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-05).pdf
Report source: Merrill Lynch (CMO)
Date: 2026-10-05
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Stronger Growth, Costlier Money
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
The latest GDP report and annual revisions show a sturdier U.S. economy than previously
reported. According to the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA), real GDP grew at a 2.2%
annualized pace in Q2 compared to 1.5% previously estimated, and Q1 was revised
from 2.1% to 2.5%. Both 2024 and 2025 were also revised slightly higher. Most of the

first half of the year revision was in categories related to the Artificial Intelligence (AI)
buildout, but real consumer spending has also been revised slightly higher, accelerating
from 0.7% annualized growth in Q1 to 3.8% in Q2 despite surging energy prices and
slow hiring.
Spending in July and August remained much stronger than labor market conditions alone
would suggest, also averaging 3.7% annualized growth. Upside revisions to net interest

income and personal saving suggest households have benefited from more income than
was previously apparent, with personal saving revised higher back to 2023. The new data,
according to the BEA, show the January to July 2026 savings rate averaged 4.8% versus
3.3% prerevision, strengthening consumer spending underpinnings despite moderate
hiring, high energy prices and rising borrowing costs.
The BEA update also included inflation methodological changes related to categories

inflated by the AI boom—such as portfolio management fees and computer software and
accessories. These improved the inflation picture to some extent—August “core” PCE
inflation was reduced by 0.3 ppts to 3% YoY, bringing it closer to “core” consumer price
index inflation, which was 2.5% YoY and 2% on a three-month annualized basis. While still
above the 2% Federal Reserve (Fed) target, “core” PCE now appears stable at 3% year over
year since January rather than accelerating. These methodological changes are expected

to dampen pass-through of AI-related price pressures into “core” inflation and effects of
stock-market swings on inflation.
They also produced an encouraging improvement in recent PCE inflation momentum.
Three-month annualized “core” PCE inflation slowed from 4.3% at the peak of tariff
effects in February to 2.1% in August. For durable goods, this rate was down from 5.8%
in February to 1.5% in August, and, for services ex-energy and healthcare, it slowed from

4.2% to 2.2%, for example. Inflation is volatile on a three-month change basis, so it
remains to be seen whether this moderation continues without further Fed tightening.
For now, the trends in the San Francisco Fed’s cyclical versus acyclical inflation measures
diverged again in August. As expected, given softening wage growth, prices most sensitive
to the labor market have eased further. At 3% YoY, this inflation category is down from
8% in early 2023 and 4.6% two years ago is back to prepandemic levels and is likely

to ease more (Exhibit 1A). In contrast, supply bottlenecks, high energy prices and other
industry-specific factors have kept acyclical inflation on an uptrend. As shown in Exhibit
1B, the relentless increase in freight costs could prolong upside pressure on this category.
The diesel market has tightened in recent months, boosting its price by 70% YoY,
consistent with Brent oil at around $200 per barrel and large reverberations across
a wide swath of goods and services prices. A government-policy-related decline in

available truck drivers is also raising transportation costs more than expected. So are
tight ocean shipping capacity and longer/more expensive voyages due to the U.S./Iran
War. Supply-chain stress also increased as companies rushed to front-run potential new
tariff increases.
On the positive side for the inflation outlook, unit labor costs (ULC) are up modestly
as robust productivity growth has absorbed much of the wage growth, limiting a major
source of domestic inflation. Upside productivity growth revisions are likely to further

reduce ULC, which could help shift focus from energy and AI-related inflation to
eventual disinflationary effects once oil and refined fuel prices start to ease. Sustained
productivity gains would support growth and margins while limiting inflation and the
need for much higher interest rates.
Investment Implications
We favor selective Equity exposure
to companies with durable demand
and pricing power, while remaining
cautious about businesses most

exposed to fuel, freight, and
financing costs. We would also
gradually increase long-duration
exposure.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Surging nominal growth has boosted corporate revenues (up 13% YoY in Q2, according to the
BEA) while contained unit labor costs helped raise margins and profits more than expected.
With labor costs declining as a share of revenues, investment-related tax incentives and
rock-bottom net interest expense, pre-tax nonfinancial corporate profits as measured in GDP
accounts rose 21% YoY in Q2. Total corporate profits after-tax were up 18.2% YoY, according to

the BEA.
Surging AI-related imports and investment are keeping the business and profits cycle on
an uptrend both here and abroad. According to BofA Global Research, the global economic
cycle backdrop improved further in September, as reflected in a broad-based increase in its
Global Wave1 indicator. Broadening U.S. manufacturing strength, fueled by ongoing AI-driven
investment, a healthier orders backlog and low inventories suggest that the expansion has

room to run—a constructive environment for earnings. Typically, a Global Wave upswing is
consistent with rising earnings revisions ratios and equity price uptrends. According to the
Global Wave, global earnings growth expectations remain exceptionally strong.
Still, absent a resolution of war-related energy and transportation disruptions, the same
industrial strength intensifies inflation pressures. A prolonged supply shock raises the risk of
restrictive interest rates and weakening economic growth. In this context, the surge in the

10-year Treasury yield above 5% is starting to reflect more than optimism about U.S. growth
and strengthening productivity outlook. Oil’s tightest recent correlation with 10-year Treasury
yields in 35 years suggests a common energy-price thread behind rising yields in developed
countries.
Bond market spillovers from normalizing Japan yields and capital repatriation are another
common thread. Higher yields on foreign sovereign bonds such as in Japan—which has

escaped stagnation and deflation by intensely pursuing investment-led growth—weaken the
relative appeal of Treasurys. That distinction matters for portfolios. A yield increase driven
mainly by durable productivity gains would come with a stronger outlook for future income
and earnings. A yield increase driven by cost shocks and greater uncertainty raises financing
costs without the same benefit.
In sum, the economy remains more resilient than expected, but the bond market is also

charging more for an uncertain inflation outlook given untethered government debt and
cooling global demand for Treasurys spurred by geopolitical tensions and Japan capital
repatriation. The case for much further tightening becomes stronger if wage growth
strengthens, productivity lags, and unit labor costs reaccelerate, causing cyclical inflation to
also pick up steam. The case becomes weaker if that measure stays contained while fuel and
freight pressures recede or rising Treasury yields reduce asset prices and economic growth.

Exhibit 1: Softer Cyclical/Higher Acyclical Inflation Keeps “Core” PCE at 3% Year Through August.
A) Cooling wage pressures ease cyclical inflation (housing, food services and accommodation,
etc.).
B) Surge in U.S. AI-related imports, tariff front-running, shipping disruptions and fuel- price
spike feed acyclical inflation.

### Page 4

4 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Yields, Yen and Iran: What Haunts the “House”
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
We recently highlighted the three-front challenge facing Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent:
engineering a stronger yen, containing the 10-year Treasury yield and securing a diplomatic
resolution to the conflict with Iran.

Bessent recently has delivered one of the year’s more memorable lines while discussing
Treasury policy and the yen: “I have asymmetric information. I am the house now.” 2 Yet several
challenges remain: the 10-year Treasury yield is near levels last seen in the early 2000s, the
yen remains structurally weaker, and a phased agreement to reopen the Strait of Hormuz
appears remote. The resulting strain on energy markets keeps inflation risks elevated.

Put differently, oil prices, Treasury yields and the dollar-yen exchange rate are all trending
against Bessent’s objectives. Below, we examine how each variable is haunting his “house.”
Haunting the “House” is the Yen. Since the U.S. and Japan mounted a record $98.7B
coordinated intervention in late July after the yen weakened to a 40-year low near 164 per
dollar, the currency has appreciated roughly 4% against the dollar. The move was further
supported on September 18 when the BoJ raised rates by 25 basis points (bps) to 1.25%,

their highest level since the mid-1990s (Exhibit 2A). However, the split 7–2 vote and Governor
Ueda’s limited forward guidance leave the path of further tightening uncertain. The key
question is whether additional BoJ hikes can produce sustained appreciation in the yen, or
merely reinforce the temporary relief provided by the summer intervention.
Bessent, meanwhile, has repeatedly signaled his preference for a stronger yen. Partly because
yen stability is also a U.S. financing concern. Persistent currency weakness could prompt

Japan to support its currency by selling foreign assets, including U.S. Treasurys—which would
be an unwelcome prospect as Washington partially relies on foreign capital flows to fund its
large fiscal deficits. That potential “haunts” the Treasury Secretary.  At precisely the moment
Washington needs foreign capital to finance its enormous fiscal deficits, it would be inopportune
for one of America’s largest creditors to become a significant seller of Treasury securities.

Haunting the “House” are Yields. The surge in yields runs counter to a White House intent
on lowering borrowing costs. The benchmark 10-year yield has broken above the 5% threshold
and climbed to levels not seen since 2007. At around 5.30%, it stands more than a full
percentage point above where it began the year (Exhibit 2B). The level is striking but so is the
speed of the move: Stronger-than-expected economic data, including a survey showing U.S.

2 September 8, 2026.
Investment Implications
While some key variables are
complicating the macro backdrop
(i.e., higher-for-longer yields/oil)
the combination of strong earnings
growth, robust consumer demand
(real final sales to private domestic
purchasers grew at 4.6% for Q2) and
ongoing capital investment suggests
that the fundamental backdrop for
U.S. Equities remains constructive.

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
business activity expanded at the fastest pace in more than five years, helped trigger an 18
bps surge in the 10-year yield—among its largest daily moves in decades. The 5-year yield also
topped 5% for the first time since 2007, while the 30-year yield reached 5.63%, its highest
level since 2002.
Compounding the move is inflation, running above the Fed’s 2% target for 67 months,

large fiscal deficits and the prospect of continued growth in Treasury issuance. Against this
backdrop, Treasury bought back $4.1 billion of long-dated debt, versus a maximum of $6
billion. That effort has done little to arrest the selloff. A subsequent auction priced nearly $4
billion of 7-year notes at 5.085%, the highest yield for that maturity in 33 years.
All in, the MOVE index, a key gauge of Treasury market volatility, jumped from 78 to 95 on

Wednesday September 23 and then to 104 the following day, its highest level since the early
days of the Iran war (Exhibit 3A).
Haunting the “House” is the Iran conflict. Early expectations that military pressure and
economic sanctions would quickly bring Iran to the negotiating table have yet to materialize.
Iranian officials have signaled that a full reopening of the Strait of Hormuz remains unlikely
while sanctions stay in place. Although talks between Washington and Tehran are continuing,

conflicting signals and lingering skepticism from both sides have kept tensions elevated.
As a result, oil prices have remained elevated in a range even as some Middle East supply
disruptions began to ease.
Startling is that Brent crude is up more than 60% this year, but refined product prices
have risen even faster. Ongoing disruptions to energy flows through the Strait of Hormuz,
combined with Ukrainian strikes on Russian refining infrastructure have tightened global

supplies of crude oil and refined fuels. Saudi Arabia has restored some drone-disrupted flows,
providing roughly 3.5 million barrels a day of relief (roughly 3% of global consumption).
The squeeze is mostly acute in diesel markets, where inventories remain constrained and
prices continue to climb (Exhibit 3B). U.S. diesel prices recently surpassed $6.50 per gallon,
the highest level on record, just as Russia has extended restrictions on diesel exports into

October. Against this backdrop, proposals to ban U.S. fuel exports risks worsening the
imbalance. According to S&P Global, an export ban could reduce refinery runs by nearly 2
million barrels per day and significantly cut gasoline production, potentially driving gasoline
and jet fuel prices even higher. As long as tensions persist, the oil impulse remains a key
impediment to structurally lower inflation.
Investment implications. Treasury Secretary Bessent increasingly sits at the intersection of

monetary policy, fiscal policy, foreign policy and national security. We remain constructive on
the U.S. economy and overweight U.S. Equities but are watching as these fronts challenge the
narrative. There is a great deal riding on the “house” versus the key macro variables of today.

A) Bond and Equity Market Volatility Diverge. B) Pain at the Pump: Diesel Prices Surge Ahead of Gasoline.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
When It Comes to Attracting Foreign Capital, the U.S. Remains Exceptional
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
There may be no better vote of confidence in a country than the willingness of
foreigners to put their money there. And by that measurement, the U.S. remains the
exception—both portfolio and FDI inflows are currently running at record highs based

on the latest data. This dynamic is one key reason why we remain constructive on U.S.
assets over the longer term.
Notwithstanding all the handwringing about America’s fiscal deficits, tariffs and rising
government debt, global investors sunk some $942B in net purchases of U.S. Equities in
the 12 months to July, the highest rolling 12-month total on record. In Q2, net purchase
of U.S. Equities soared to $426B, up 62% from the same period a year ago (Exhibit 4A).

By country, flows were down from China—not surprisingly—but strong from Taiwan and
South Korea, who have taken to recycling their massive AI-generated trade surplus back
into the U.S. In the end, strong portfolio inflows suggest that international investors
remain willing to increase their exposure to the world's largest capital market—that’s
bullish for U.S. assets.
Meanwhile, Exhibit 4B highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be

inside the U.S., with FDI inflows totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year.
That is a staggering 103% jump from a year ago and means FDI inflows are on track for
a record year, estimated at $480B for 2026.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Two, within the $32 trillion-plus economy with extraordinarily consumer

purchasing power, foreign firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment
than just trade (exports). Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers,
semiconductors, power generation, etc., has created numerous potential investment
opportunities for many foreign firms that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging
capex cycle. Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly
developed technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.

In the end, double-barreled capital inflows speak to the absolute and relative
attractiveness of the U.S. economy and capital markets. Stay invested in America—the
rest of the world is.

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

ICE BofA MOVE Index is a market-implied measure of U.S. bond market volatility.
Consumer Price Index measures the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a market basket of consumer goods and services.
Baltic Dry Index is a daily shipping and trade index published by the Baltic Exchange in London that measures the cost of moving raw materials like iron ore, coal, and grain by sea.
Volatility Index (VIX) is a real-time market indicator that measures the stock market's expected volatility over the next 30 days.

Bank of America, Merrill, their affiliates and advisors do not provide legal, tax or accounting advice. Clients should consult their legal and/or tax advisors before making any financial decisions.
This material does not take into account a client’s particular investment objectives, financial situations, or needs and is not intended as a recommendation, offer, or solicitation for the purchase
or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

of America, Merrill or any affiliate to sell or provide, or a specific invitation for a consumer to apply for, any particular retail financial product or service that may be available.
The Chief Investment Office (“CIO”) provides thought leadership on wealth management, investment strategy and global markets; portfolio management solutions; due diligence; and solutions
oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.
BofA Global Research is research produced by BofA Securities, Inc. (“BofAS”) and/or one or more of its affiliates. BofAS is a registered broker-dealer, Member SIPC and wholly owned subsidiary

of Bank of America Corporation.
All recommendations must be considered in the context of an individual investor’s goals, time horizon, liquidity needs and risk tolerance. Not all recommendations will be in the best interest
of all investors.
Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh 
 Biwase là doanh nghiệp hàng đầu 
trong lĩnh vực cung cấp nước sạch với 
thị phần đạt 100% tại Bình Dương. 
Ngoài ra, công ty còn mở rộng sang 
lĩnh vực đầu tư nhà máy xử lý nước và 
chất thải rắn nhằm hoàn thiện hệ 
sinh thái dịch vụ hạ tầng môi trường 
tại khu vực. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Nắng nóng thúc đẩy tiêu thụ nước sạch khối dân cư, đem về tổng doanh thu cấp nước tại Bình Dương và 
Bình Phước năm 2026/2027 đạt 2,953/3,192 tỷ VND (6.8%/7.4%). 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Nhà máy nước Nhị Thành giai đoạn 3 đi vào hoạt động giúp tổng doanh thu nhà máy đạt lần lượt 213/247 tỷ 
VND (+16%/16% YoY) trong 2026/2027 và duy trì tăng trưởng trung bình 15% đến 2030. 
 Tham khảo trong báo cáo kỳ trước: 
tại đây

2026/2027, tương ứng mức tăng 34%/15%

Doanh thu 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ do 
không còn ghi nhận doanh thu 1 lần 
từ phí nước thải; trong khi LNST 
công ty mẹ tăng trưởng 1 chữ số 
 Doanh thu 2Q2026 đạt 1,222 tỷ (-8% YoY/18.1% QoQ) và LNST công ty mẹ đạt 
325 tỷ VND (7% YoY/55% QoQ). Mảng cung cấp nước tăng trưởng ổn định, tiếp 
tục là trụ cột chính của BWE, đóng góp doanh thu 785 tỷ VND (+12% YoY/12% 
QoQ) nhờ nhà máy nước Long An vận hành từ Q3/2025. Kinh doanh tăng trưởng 
tích cực là nhờ giá nước đầu vào mua từ TDM thấp hơn giúp tăng biên lợi nhuận 
gộp, cùng với không phát sinh lỗ tỷ giá như cùng kỳ năm ngoái.

Tiêu thụ nước khu vực dân sinh 
được thúc đẩy nhờ thời tiết nắng 
nóng hơn; trong khi triển vọng 
nhóm khách hàng công nghiệp bị 
ảnh hưởng bởi bất lợi thuế quan 
 Dự báo đồng thuận xác suất xảy ra El Nino ở mức xấp xỉ 100% từ 2H2026 kéo 
dài đến tháng đầu 2027 cho thấy cường độ và thời gian kéo dài của các đợt 
nắng nóng sẽ gay gắt và kéo dài hơn so với các năm trước. Đây là cơ sở để 
chúng tôi điều chỉnh tăng tăng trưởng tiêu thụ nước nhóm khách hàng dân cư 
tại Bình Dương và Bình Phước trong năm 2026 - 2027 lên mức 7.5/6.5% YoY 
(+2.6/1.1 ppts). Trái lại, sau khi section 301 được công bố và có hiệu lực thay 
thế section 122, tình hình thuế quan có diễn biến bất lợi trong ngắn hạn cho 
các ngành xuất khẩu truyền thống của Việt Nam. Cụ thể, Section 301 không tự 
hết hạn và có nền tảng pháp lý chắc chắn (ít nhất là trong nhiệm kỳ của 
Trump); đồng thời áp dụng hạn ngạch thuế quan cho một lượng hàng dệt may 
nhất định của một số nước không có Việt Nam, khiến bối cảnh bất lợi cho các 
ngành sản xuất truyền thống. Tuy nhiên, với việc Bình Dương đang tập trung 
đầu tư hạ tầng khu công nghiệp hiện đại nhằm thu hút dòng vốn FDI vào các 
lĩnh vực công nghệ cao, sản lượng nước sạch sẽ được duy trì khi tiêu thụ khối 
sản xuất truyền thống được bù đắp bởi nhóm công nghệ cao. Theo đó, trong 
năm 2026, KBSV điều chỉnh tăng nhẹ dự báo sản lượng nước sạch tại Bình 
Dương & Bình Phước đạt 224.9 triệu m3 (+6.8% YoY), với mức tăng của nhóm 
sản xuất/sinh hoạt/hành chính lần lượt là 5.8%/7.5%/11% YoY.

Biểu đồ 4. Xác suất tăng dần của EL Nino

Nguồn: Bloomberg, KBSV tổng hợp

Mảng cấp nước: Tiến độ điều chỉnh 
giá bán nước gián đoạn do môi 
trường lạm phát leo cao 
 Do tác động của cuộc chiến Mỹ - Iran gây ra gián đoạn dòng chảy vận tải dầu 
tại eo biển Hormuz, giá nhiên liệu trong nước đã tăng mạnh từ đầu năm (Dầu 
Diesel - +64.3%, E5 RON 92 – +40%) và khiến cho tình hình lạm phát trong nước 
diễn biến tiêu cực: CPI bình quân 8 tháng năm 2026 tăng 4.5% với chỉ số giá 
nhóm nhà ở, điện nước, chất đốt và vật liệu xây dựng tăng cao nhất với 6.7% và 
giao thông tăng 5.4%. Trong bối cảnh đó, chúng tôi cho rằng tiến độ điều 
chỉnh giá bán nước tại các thị trường của BWE có khả năng bị đẩy lùi sang 
2027-2028 hoặc cho đến khi tình hình giá nhiên liệu ổn định trở lại. Trong dài 
hạn, khả năng đàm phán giá nước thành công là khá cao bởi: i) Thông tư 
145/2025/TT-BTC có hiệu lực từ 2026 là căn cứ để UBND quy định tỷ suất lợi 
nhuận định mức các dự án nhà máy nước; ii) BWE và cổ đông lớn là TDM dự 
kiến đầu tư và mở rộng công suất cung cấp – phân phối nước tại các vùng hẻo 
lánh nên có khả năng tăng áp lực đàm phán tăng giá nước tại Bình Dương. 
Trong kịch bản thận trọng, KBSV điều chỉnh tăng giá nước sạch trung bình khu

vực Bình Dương 1%/năm từ 2028 – 2030.

Đơn giá xử thu gom, vận chuyện và 
xử lý rác thải được phê duyệt cho 
phép cải thiện doanh thu và lợi 
nhuận của mảng này 
 UBND TP.HCM (bao gồm TP.HCM cũ, Bình Dương, Bà Rịa Vũng Tàu) quy định 
giá cụ thể đối với dịch vụ vận chuyển, xử lý chất thải rắn sinh hoạt trên địa bàn. 
Theo đó, quy định mới thay thế văn bản có hiệu lực từ 2016 sẽ chuyển hình 
thức tính giá đầy đủ theo 3 cấu phần: Thu gom, vận chuyển và Xử lý; với khung 
giá được xác định cho các phường đô thị và xã/nông thôn. So với quy định 
2016, quy định mới điều chỉnh tăng mạnh khung giá khoán đối với chủ nguồn 
thải nhỏ (Hộ gia đình tăng 80-184% đối với khu vực đô thị, 55-140% đối với khu 
vực nông thôn; Hộ kinh doanh nhỏ lẻ tăng 42-150% đối với khu vực đô thị, 20-
138% đối với khu vực nông thôn); đồng thời yêu cầu chủ thải tại nguồn và chủ 
nguồn thải lớn áp dụng khung giá thành (bao gồm giá thu gom, vận chuyển và 
xử lý) nhằm phản ánh đầy đủ và chính xác hơn chi phí liên quan tới các khâu xử 
lý rác thải thực tế. Chúng tôi cho rằng việc ban hành khung giá mới tính toán 
đầy đủ và chính xác hơn các chi phí phát sinh phản ánh định hướng phát triển 
hạ tầng xử lý rác thải hiện đại của TP.HCM, đặt trọng tâm vào các nhà máy đốt 
rác phát điện thân thiện và an toàn với môi trường. Đối với doanh nghiệp hoạt 
động trong lĩnh vực môi trường nhiều kinh nghiệm như BWE, cơ chế thu gom, 
vận chuyển và xử lý minh bạch sẽ giúp thúc đẩy hiệu quả các dự án mà doanh 
nghiệp có kế hoạch triển khai trong tương lai. KBSV dự báo doanh thu và lợi 
nhuận gộp của mảng xử lý rác thải lần lượt đạt 1,069/1,234 và 165.7/180.7 tỷ 
VND lần lượt trong 2026/2027, tương ứng mức tăng 34%/15% và 27%/9% YoY.

Biwase thành công tăng vốn cổ 
phần lên 2,513 tỷ VND 
 Sau đợt chào báo cho cổ đông hiện hữu với giá chào bán 37,000 đồng/cổ 
phiếu, doanh nghiệp dự kiến huy động được khoảng 1,162.5 tỷ VND, nâng vốn 
điều lệ lên mức 2,513 tỷ VND. Khoản vốn bổ sung được dự kiến sử dụng cho ba 
mục đích chính: thanh toán nợ vay đến hạn (khoảng 773.3 tỷ đồng), đầu tư dự 
án nhà máy điện rác công suất 24MW (225 tỷ đồng) và mua cổ phần tại CTCP 
Cấp nước Ninh Thuận (hơn 164 tỷ đồng), qua đó biến doanh nghiệp này trở 
thành công ty liên kết. Dự kiến, lợi nhuận đóng góp từ công ty liên kết trong 
2026/2027 tiếp tục tăng trưởng tích cực, đạt 238.1/245.2 tỷ VND (40%/3% YoY).

Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 
59,000 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Về diễn biến thị trường, BWE hiện đang giao dịch quanh mức -1 độ lệch chuẩn 
trung bình 5 năm EV/EBITDA, phản ánh những bất ổn trong thương mại toàn 
cầu ảnh hưởng tới tiêu thụ nước phục vụ sản xuất. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi đánh 
giá triển vọng trong tương lai của mảng môi trường (bao gồm nước thải và rác 
thải) cộng với chiến lược đa dạng hóa địa bàn cung cấp nước sẽ bù đắp được 
KQKD tăng trưởng chậm lại.

Kết hợp 2 phương pháp định giá FCFF và EV/EBITDA, KBSV khuyến nghị MUA 
với BWE, giá mục tiêu 59,000 VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương với mức lợi suất kỳ 
vọng 42.2% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 22/09/2026. Mức EV/EBITDA mục tiêu cho 
việc định giá doanh nghiệp là 8.8 lần, tương ứng với mức trung bình 5 năm của 
doanh nghiệp, để phản ánh sự cải thiện KQKD đến từ mảng môi trường và cơ 
chế giảm giá mua nước từ TDM sẽ bù đắp sự giảm tốc của mảng cấp nước trong 
ngắn hạn. Với mức giá mục tiêu trên, cổ phiếu được định giá tương đương P/B 
1.7x cho 2026, phù hợp với việc ROE được kỳ vọng cải thiện lên mức 14.1%.

Document 240: First Impressions_ NZ household incomes, assets and savings update - June quarter 2026 (2026-10-18)

Source PDF: all report pdf/First Impressions_ NZ household incomes, assets and savings update - June quarter 2026 (2026-10-18).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-18
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

#consumer #newzealand
First Impressions: NZ household incomes, assets and savings
update - June quarter 2026
By Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
10:24 October 08 2026 Share
New Zealand incomes and savings are rising, as is the value of households’ financial
assets. However, the value of housing assets remains flat.
Key takeouts from Stats NZ’s latest update on household incomes, assets and savings (June quarter
2026)

### Page 2

Household incomes
Disposable income levels in the economy grew by 5.2% in the year to June, down from 6.5% in the
year to March

Savings and wealth
Household savings have continued to rise, climbing by $2.5b over the past quarter.

Overall household wealth levels were up 1.9% over the past year. Under the surface, the make-up of
household wealth continues to shift.

Interest costs 
Overall household income levels are continuing to rise. Much of that is due to increases in
entrepreneurial earnings (which is in part due to increased earnings in the agricultural sector). Wage
and salary growth has been more limited but has picked up.
Household savings levels have been rising for three years now, with another solid rise over the past
three months. 
Overall household wealth levels have risen modestly. Although the value of households’ financial
assets is continuing to rise, housing assets have essentially shown no growth since late 2023.
Household spending on interest costs rose just slightly in the June quarter. That’s the first increase in
two years. The easing in average borrowing costs seen over the past few years has come to a close
with the RBNZ’s hiking cycle now in train.
Underpinning the rise in overall income levels have been continued increases in entrepreneurial
earnings, which are up 12% over the past year. That will include stronger earnings in the agricultural
sector, which has been boosted by the strength in commodity export prices. That lift in agricultural
earnings has been reflected in stronger economic confidence and spending in many rural regions.
We expect commodity export earnings will remain firm over the year ahead. 
In contrast, wage and salary earnings are up just 2.7% over the past year. That’s up from 1.8% in the
year to March, but it’s a fairly muted rise, especially given the continued increases in the population
and inflation of 4.1%.
June’s large increase in savings builds on the solid increases seen over the past three years.
The savings rate in the June quarter was 3.7% of disposable incomes, down just slightly from last
quarter.
The value of households’ financial assets has continued to rise and is up 4.6% over the past year. 
However, the value of housing and land assets is down 0.9% over the past year, and it has remained
essentially unchanged since late 2023. We expect housing market conditions will remain soft over
the coming year, limiting increases in overall household wealth. 
Households’ spending on interest costs rose just slightly in the June quarter, climbing by 0.3%.
While that’s a relatively small change, it is the first increase that we’ve seen in two years.

### Page 3

Related articles

Media Contact:
Satish Ranchhod, Senior Economist
P: +64 9 336 5668
M: +64 21 710 852
Over the past few years, many borrowers saw their debt servicing costs fall as they rolled off earlier
high mortgage rates and onto lower ones. That helped to support household spending.
However, the easing in average borrowing cost has now ended. Recent months have seen mortgage
rates pushing higher as the RBNZ has begun to hike the Official Cash Rate, with further hikes
expected over the coming year. 
Looking ahead, we don’t expect a significant increase in average household borrowing costs.
However, we won’t see the same support for spending that we did over the past few years.

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

People Bank of China (October 20)

We expect the PBOC to keep the 1Y LPR 
unchanged next week . Industrial 
production is expected to edge up from 
5.2% yoy in August to 5.3% in September, 
supported by firm external demand. 
Exports stayed strong, rising 36.3% yoy 
in September, driven by the regional AI -
electronics upcycle.

Domestic momentum, however, remains 
uneven. Retail sales growth is expected 
to moderate to 0.3% yoy  in September. 
Household sentiment is weak amid 
uncertain job prospects, slower income 
growth and elevated precautionary 
savings, while subdued property prices 
continue to weigh on household wealth 
and consumption. Investment also stays 
weak, with fixed as set investment 
expected to contract further by 7.5% yoy 
ytd in September. Corporate investment 
is cautious amid the anti -involution 
campaign and geopolitical tensions, 
while the property sector continues to be 
a major drag.

Policymakers are likely to maintain a 
measured policy stance, relying on 
targeted fiscal support to sustain growth 
rather than further rate cuts.

Bank Indonesia and Bangko Sentral ng 
Pilipinas (Oct 21 and 22).

Bank Indonesia is expected to extend its 
pause, as they lean on non-rate measures, 
including intervention and hedging 
facilities to help the currency.  On Friday, 
MSCI signaled a  potential shift in 
Indonesia’s status to a standalone 
status, rather than a reclassification to 
frontier market , after consultations are 
complete in mid -2027. Any ensuing risk -
off move in the currency , would likely 
prompt greater central bank vigilance 
and intervention.

Our base case for the benchmark rate to 
be held unchanged this quarter , backed 
by a lower conviction around back -to-
back US rate hikes in Oct and relative 
rupiah stability. Separately, the 
government announced additional 
support for low-income households in 4Q 
by way of in -kind assistance and wage 
subsidies. This year’s fiscal deficit i s 
likely to close in at -2.85% of GDP, close 
to our forecast at -2.9%, but few 
uncertainties facing the 2027 math (note).

We expect a 25bp hike from the BSP to 
5.25% in October , after three rate 
increases earlier in the year. Rising by the 
highest pace in Asia, Philippines inflation 
surged to 7.2% y/y in September from 6.1% 
in August, matching this year's peak and 
approaching a three -year high.  Inflation 
risks remain tilted to the upside, driven by 
further public transport fare adjustments, 
elevated oil prices, recent wage hikes 
and peso depreciation. Fiscal space is, 
meanwhile, limited to support 
households, via subsidies and/ instate 
price caps. Governor Remolona also 
signalled that the BSP remains ready to 
act on rates to contain inflation. With 
inflationary pressures likely to be 
persistent, the likelihood of further hikes 
in 4Q26 rises.

### Page 3

The Week Ahead October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

Bank of Korea (October 22)

The Bank of Korea is expected to hold its 
policy rate at 3.00%, following back -to-
back rate hikes in July and August. AI -
driven growth momentum remains strong, 
as reflected in September export growth 
of around 80% YoY. However, the 
urgency for further tightening has eased, 
with CPI inflation stabilising at 2.9% YoY 
in September. The recent KOSPI 
correction and continued KRW strength 
also reduce the need for another near -
term hike. We maintain our call for a final 
25bp rate hike in November, bringing the 
policy rate to 3.25% by year-end.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Japan

September trade and CPI data are due 
shortly. Export growth is expected to 
remain solid at 24% YoY, supported by 
continued strength in AI -related 
semiconductor exports. CPI inflation is 
projected to rise notably to 2.6% YoY, 
driven by the phase -out of publ ic utility 
subsidies, higher global energy prices, 
and persistent inflationary pressures 
from a weak yen. The BOJ is likely to 
increasingly acknowledge that its 2% 
inflation target is within reach. However, 
it is expected to keep rates unchanged 
at 1.25% at its October 29–30 meeting to 
assess the impact of the latest rate hike 
in September.

Taiwan

September export orders and industrial 
production data are due shortly. Export 
orders are expected to sustain 60% YoY 
growth, while industrial production is 
projected to expand by 20% YoY. The 
global AI boom faces growing headwinds 
from hyperscalers’ cash flow pressures, 
central bank rate hikes, and higher bond 
yields. However, our high -frequency 
indicators across AI demand (token 
expenditure), investment (hyperscaler  
capex and data centre construction 
spending), and supply (semiconductor 
sales and exports) show no clear signs of 
a significant slowdown so far.

Malaysia

Malaysia’s external demand likely 
remained strong in September 2026, 
while inflation edged up slightly. We 
expect export growth to sustain its 
robust expansion at 45% yoy , with 
momentum anchored by strong demand 
for electrical and electronics goods. Oil 
& gas shipments likely remained buoyant. 
However, high base effects prevented a 
faster pace of expansion.

We expect headline inflation to rise 
modestly to 2.1% yoy in September 2026. 
The renewed increase in global oil prices 
likely contributed to higher increases in 
domestic energy costs, although fuel 
subsidies continued to limit the cost 
pass-through and keep underlying 
inflationary pressures contained. Food 
inflation likely also firmed, with adverse 
weather conditions associated with El 
Nino posing upside risks.

### Page 4

The Week Ahead October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         4

Singapore

We expect Singapore’s core and 
headline inflation to edge up slightly to 
2.3% yoy and 2.4% yoy , respectively, in 
September 2026, following modest 
increases to 2.2% yoy and 2.3% yoy in 
August. Inflationary pressures likely 
reflected firm private transport price 
increases amid higher petrol prices 
despite slower car price growth , the 
ongoing pass -through of higher global 
energy prices to fuel -exposed services 
items, positive contribution from 
elevated electricity & gas prices, firmer 
food inflation, and a slight increase in 
still-muted accommodation inflation. 
Firm, albeit not acc elerating, inflation in 
September 2026, which may be close to 
its peak in the Monetary Authority of 
Singapore’s assessment, likely validated 
its decision to maintain SGD NEER policy 
settings at the October 2026 review, 
following two proactive tightening 
moves in April and July.

Hong Kong

CPI is projected to edge up from 1.7% yoy 
in August to 1.9% in September, 
supported by improving consumption 
sentiment and higher global energy 
prices. Mainland visitor arrivals 
increased by 29.0% yoy in September on 
a daily average basis, providing furthe r 
support to retail spending and 
consumer-related services. Meanwhile, 
renewed conflict in the Middle East 
during the month has pushed up global 
energy prices, adding to upward 
pressure on utility and transport costs.

### Page 5

5 5 5
營運成果回顧暨未來展望
5
1H26營收再創同期新高，AI產品組合升級帶動獲利提升：受惠於高階AI應用需求持續成長，1H26營
收達新台幣892億元，年增13.9%，再創歷年同期新高。其中，伺服器／光模塊／ IC載板合計營收年
增113%，營收占比達21.6%。隨高附加價值產品比重增加，1H26毛利率及營業利益率分別提升至
22.4%及7.0%，顯示公司成長動能正由營收規模擴張，進一步轉化為獲利提升。
1
2 AI算力建設開啟高階PCB長期成長週期，臻鼎憑藉完整技術布局邁入新一輪成長：隨下一世代AI平台
陸續量產，公司伺服器／光模塊業務自第二季起逐季增強，2026年相關營收目標維持成倍成長。中
長期而言，AI需求將由伺服器與光模塊進一步擴展至Edge AI及人形機器人等終端應用，持續提升高
階PCB的使用量、技術門檻與產品價值，公司對2026-2030 年的成長深具信心。
3 以業界最大的擴產規模打造下一階段營運動能：高階PCB產能建置與客戶認證均需較長前置期，唯
有糧草先行，才能掌握AI帶來的長期成長機會。公司2026年資本支出為新台幣800億+，2027-2028年

亦將維持較高投資水準，聚焦iHDI ／ mSAP、HLC、IC載板及高階軟板等高成長、高附加價值領域。
公司目前共有22棟新廠建設中、9棟新廠規劃中，新增產能將依客戶導入及量產進度，於2026至
2030年間分階段開出，逐步將資本投入轉化為營收與獲利貢獻。
4 禮鼎於2026年7月2日正式向港交所遞交主板上市申請：上市申請目前仍處於審查程序，相關資訊皆
以港交所正式刊載的文件為準。

### Page 22

全球生產基地加速擴產，效益將於2026年起逐步顯現
◼ 泰國一廠：
◼ 主要生產高階iHDI、HLC及光模塊產品，於3Q25進入量產。
◼ 量產爬坡順利，已通過多家客戶認證及量產，尚有多家伺服器與光模塊全球領先客戶正積極進行認證中。
◼ 7月已達損益兩平。
◼ 目前另有三棟新廠房及一棟機械鑽孔中心同步建置中，規劃將於4Q26起逐步進入試產階段。
◼ 淮安園區：
◼ 規劃至2026年底，淮安HA、HB、HC園區將有6棟高階PCB新廠完成建設。
◼ 另HD園區已於今年四月動土，規劃新建HDI/MSAP與HLC廠，8月10日封頂，預計明年三月加入生產。
◼ 深圳園區：
◼ 第三園區智慧製造基地於今年七月動土，預計興建三棟智慧製造廠房，重點布局AI伺服器用及光模塊用的
高階類載板，及AI終端用高階軟板等高端產品。
◼ 禮鼎深圳第二園區於今年九月正式動土，總投資金額不低於人民幣120億元，規劃建設數座工廠，聚焦
FCBGA等高階IC載板。新產能主要面向AI、高效能運算及先進封裝需求，進一步強化公司高階FCBGA產品的

研發、製造與規模化交付能力。

Document 348: US MARKET CALL_ Yes, AI Is Too Big To Fail

Source PDF: all report pdf/US MARKET CALL_ Yes, AI Is Too Big To Fail.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-17
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: US MARKET CALL: Yes, AI Is Too Big To Fail
Date:    3 hours ago
US MARKET CALL: Yes, AI Is Too Big To Fail

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
US MARKET CALL: Yes, AI Is
Too Big To Fail
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 17 Oct 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Nils Rasmusson / Unsplash
I. Bonds
We've all been suffering from AI fatigue for a while. The daily news about it
has been head-spinning. We've been warned that AI is a bubble that will
burst, just as the late-1990s tech bubble led to the Tech Wreck in the early
2000s. Analogies to the credit excesses of the Great Financial Crisis have

### Page 3

also been proffered. Last week's rout in AI-related stocks convinced the
naysayers that the AI bubble is bursting, just as they’ve long predicted it
would.
Our happier spin is that the Bond Vigilantes are now doing a good job of
regulating and allocating capital to the AI ecosystem. Credit-quality
spreads are widening to reflect the riskiness of the bonds issued by some
of the dodgier AI-related borrowers like CoreWeave and Oracle. The
rapidly rising cost of credit for such borrowers should deflate the AI bubble

without bursting it. If some AI-related credits do blow up, the Godfathers of
the AI mafia (along with their protectors in the government) will clean up
the mess. As in "The Godfather II," Michael Corleone has too much at
stake to let his brother Fredo screw up the family business.
Meanwhile, the rout in the AI corporate bond market has triggered a flight
to quality and safety that has stopped the rout in the US Treasury bond
market (chart). This confirms our thesis that the recent rise in US bond

yields mostly reflects better-than-expected economic growth. Tougher
credit conditions in the AI corporate bond market should curb some of the
AI excesses. However, we doubt this will trigger an economy-wide credit
crunch or a recession. We aren't convinced that it will slow capital
spending much, since labor and materials shortages, along with public
NIMBY pushback, have already slowed the pace of data center
construction.
II. S&P Earnings

### Page 4

The big banks reported mixed but solid overall Q3 earnings last week. On
average, they met banking industry analysts' expectations. Last week, the
consensus EPS for all analysts covering S&P 500 companies edged up to
$365.70 this year, $424.19 next year, and $492.62 in 2028 (chart). Those
are extremely bullish expectations considering that we are still forecasting
$500 in 2030! If the analysts are right, the S&P 500 could rise to 10,000
by the end of next year, assuming a forward P/E of 20.0-21.0. For now, we

are sticking with 10,000 by the end of the Roaring 2020s, i.e., at the end
of 2029.
Last week, Q3's consensus EPS growth rate remained at 24.0% y/y, while
the Q4 estimate rose to 29.8% (chart). We've been among the most
bullish investment strategists on the Street, but analysts are still ahead of
us.

### Page 5

The S&P 500, S&P 400, and S&P 600 continued to rise to record highs
last week (chart).
III. Stock Valuation
The recent stock market selloff has been driven by falling forward P/Es
even as forward earnings have been rising to record highs (chart). The
former are nearing the troughs they made during last year's "Liberation
Day" lows.
Since roughly mid-2025, rising FEMO (fabulous earnings momentum) has
more than offset falling FOMO (fear of missing out) (chart). Since October

6, the reverse has been true: Falling FOMO is outpacing the rise in
FEMO.

### Page 6

IV. Performance
The stock market had a tough week last week. Leading the 1.95%
pullback was MAGS (down 3.89%), while XMAGS fell 1.38% (chart). The
Russell 2000 fell 2.4%, while the Nasdaq fell 4.5%. The DJIA rose 0.67%
for the week. The Impressive 493 is still outperforming the Magnificent-7
on a year-to-date basis, with a gain of 14.1% versus 10.2%.
V. Sentiment
The market letter writers have been more bullish than retail investors
lately (chart). Odds are they will both be a bit more pessimistic this week.

### Page 7

Only 47.4% of the S&P 500 are trading above their 200-day moving
averages (chart).
VI. Commodities
Aluminum prices are falling after matching their 2022 peak. This suggests
that supply-chain disruptions attributable to Gulf War III are receding, as
they had done after Russia invaded Ukraine (chart).

### Page 8

Copper prices remained near their recent record high last week despite
chatter that tightening credit conditions could slow the AI buildout and
weigh on overall GDP growth (chart).
The outlook for higher-for-longer diesel prices offers no relief (chart).

### Page 9

We aren't giving up on gold yet. The current correction resembles
previous ones that occurred in the middle of significant gold bull runs
(chart). This time, rising interest rates have been weighing on the metal's
price. However, significant buying by the People's Bank of China and
other central banks isn't interest-rate sensitive. We are still aiming for a
gold price of $5,000 an ounce by the end of this year or early next year.
💡

log in to the Yardeni QuickTakes website and post them at the end
of the QuickTakes article. Paid members' contributions may be
featured in our segment, "Ed Answers Your Questions".

《天然氣價格：美持平或略低於去年 ，歐洲偏上行 》
美國鑽井活動緩升 ，增加較多在天然氣 ，隨美國新
的液化天然氣出口工廠上線 ，逐漸改善管道瓶頸 ，
天然氣 10月庫存僅高於五年均值 2%，注入期可持
續增至11月中，預估今年冬天暖氣度日數將持平去
年 ， 並 比 過 去 五 個 冬 季 的 均 值 低1% ， 預估美國
4Q26天然氣價格將因季節性走升，但持平或略低於
去年。卡達能源LNG船已出口 ，但非往歐洲 ，歐洲
儲氣量雖拉升 ，但僅達 70%，遠低於過往 ，TTF價
格須優於亞洲以吸引運往亞洲的 LNG轉往歐洲 ，加

上中東地緣緊張，預期4Q26 TTF價格仍有上行風險。
重點摘要
• 天然氣：美國天然氣出口增
加，庫存增速下降 ，使4Q26
美國天然氣價格持平或略低
於去年同期；歐洲因庫存偏
低的回補需求將使天然氣價

長，維持股東回報 ，較看好
綜合油企。

Document 424: フランス予算審議、極右がキングメーカーに浮上 ～大統領選を前に崩れる社会党との協力関係～

Source PDF: all report pdf/フランス予算審議、極右がキングメーカーに浮上 ～大統領選を前に崩れる社会党との協力関係～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

Europe Trends

2026 年 10 月２日

1/3 
フランス予算審議、極右がキングメーカーに浮上 
～大統領選を前に崩れる社会党との協力関係～ 
 経済調査部 首席エコノミスト  田中 理 
（℡：050-5474-7494）

（要旨） 
○ フランス政府は１日に閣議決定した 2027 年予算案で、財政再建を軌道に戻すため、新たに
430 億ユーロの財政改善措置を盛り込んだ。だが、少数与党のルコルニュ政権には予算を成
立させる議会基盤がなく、前年に予算成立で協力した穏健左派政党の社会党は、大統領選を
前に政権との対決姿勢を強めている。代わって予算成立の鍵を握りつつあるのが、大統領選
挙での勝利を目指す極右政党の国民連合（ＲＮ）だ。今後の焦点は、年金抑制や増税・歳出
削減を巡る修正を通じ、社会党またはＲＮの不信任回避を取り付けられるかに移る。

■430 億ユーロの追加財政措置を盛り込んだ政府予算案 
フランス政府は10 月１日、2027 年予算案を閣議決定した。2026 年に国内総生産（ＧＤＰ）比
で 5.4％程度に膨らむ見通しの財政赤字を、2027 年に 5.0％へ縮小することを目指す。財政再建を
軌道に戻すには540 億ユーロの財政改善措置が必要とされ、既に決定済みの措置に加えて、2027
年予算案で新たに430 億ユーロを盛り込んだ。このうちの約６割は歳出抑制を通じて捻出すると
しており、公務員給与の算定基準の凍結、国防など一部の優先分野を除く省庁予算の削減、地方
政府への歳出削減要求、年金給付の物価連動の一部停止、医療費の伸び抑制、疾病手当や労災給
付の厳格化などを実施する。歳入面では、年金受給者向け所得控除の縮小、企業向け社会保険料
負担軽減措置の縮小、糖分含有量の多い加工食品への課税強化などを盛り込んだ。家計や企業を
対象とした一律の大型増税は見送ったが、税・社会保険料負担のＧＤＰ比は現在の43.9％から
2027 年に44.2％へ上昇する見通しだ。

■前年の予算成立を支えた社会党は態度を硬化 
予算審議は難航が避けられない。９月 24 日付けレポート「難航するフランスの予算協議」で指
摘した通り、ルコルニュ首相が率いる政府は国民議会（下院）で過半数を持たず、予算成立には
野党の協力が必要となる。憲法 49 条３項に基づく手続きを用いれば、議会で予算法案そのものを
採決することなく成立させることができるが、野党は内閣不信任案を提出できる。不信任案が否
決されれば法案は採択されたものと見做される一方、可決されれば内閣は総辞職する。 
前年の予算協議では、予算未成立のまま越年した後、穏健左派の野党・社会党（ＰＳ）の協力

### Page 2

2/3 
で予算成立に漕ぎ着けた。社会党は、マクロン大統領の目玉政策である年金改革の停止などを条
件に、49 条３項発動後の内閣不信任案への賛成を見送り、予算成立と政権存続を容認した。今回
発表された予算案には、社会党が求める富裕層や高収益企業への課税強化などが盛り込まれなか
った一方で、年金や社会保障を中心に幅広い負担を求める措置が含まれている。社会党のフォー
ル第一書記は政府の予算案について、 「何も変わらなければ不信任にする」と警告している。2027
年春の大統領選挙が近づくなか、社会党が再び予算成立と政権存続に協力する政治的なハードル
は前年より高まっている。政府が再び社会党の不信任回避を取り付けるには、予算案の大幅な修
正が必要になる。

■予算成立のキングメーカーに浮上する国民連合 
こうしたなか、予算成立の鍵を握りつつあるのが、大統領選挙での勝利を目指す極右政党・国
民連合（ＲＮ）だ。ＲＮの大統領候補であるルペン氏は、年金の物価連動を抑制する措置などに
反対しており、今回の政府案に反対票を投じる可能性を示唆している。同時に、 「不完全な予算で
あっても、予算がないよりマシ」との趣旨の発言を示唆しており、予算成立そのものを阻止しな
い可能性も示唆している。政府が49 条３項を発動した場合でも、他の野党が提出する内閣不信任
案への賛成を見送る可能性がある。 
ＲＮは財政再建そのものを否定している訳ではなく、年金受給者や現役世代への負担増に反対
する代わりに、政府機関の経費削減、外国人向け給付の削減、欧州連合（ＥＵ）への拠出金削減
などを要求している。政府案には、外国人が一部の社会給付を受給する際の居住要件を厳しくす
る措置など、ＲＮの主張と重なるものも含まれる。政府側はＲＮへの譲歩との見方を否定してい
るものの、ＲＮが予算成立を左右する可能性が高まっている。

■不信任回避に向けた与野党協議が本格化 
予算案は10 月中旬から国民議会の本会議で本格的な審議に入る。例年の審議日程を考えると、
まずは歳入部分を審議し、その後に歳出部分や社会保障予算の審議が続き、11 月中旬にかけて国
民議会での審議が進められ、年内の成立を目指すことになる。この１ヶ月余りが、ルコルニュ政
権にとって野党との妥協点を探る重要な期間となる。まずは議会審議を通じて野党の修正案を部
分的に取り込み、そのうえで通常採決を目指すのか、49 条３項を用いるかを判断することになる
だろう。予算案そのものへの積極的な賛成を取り付けることが難しい場合も、社会党かＲＮの少
なくとも一方が内閣不信任に加わらない内容まで予算案を修正できるかどうかが重要となる。 
焦点の１つが年金給付の抑制だ。政府案では、低額年金を物価連動停止の対象外とする一方、
月額 1260 ユーロを上回る年金については物価連動を停止する。年金抑制を緩和する代わりに富裕
層や大企業への課税を強化する方向であれば社会党との距離を縮めやすくなり、歳出削減を上積
みし、外国人向け給付などを厳格化する方向であればＲＮの要求に近づく。両党が求める修正の
方向性は大きく異なり、政府は限られた財政余地のなかで難しい選択を迫られる。

### Page 3

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
■予算審議が映す大統領選前の政治力学 
政府にはもう１つの非常手段も残されている。憲法47 条では、議会が予算案の提出から70 日
以内に審議を終えなければ、政府は予算案の規定を政令（オルドナンス）によって実施すること
が認められている。今回の場合、12 月17 日がその期限となる。現在の政治体制（第五共和制）の
下でこの手法が実際に使われた例はなく、実施すれば大きな政治的な反発を招く可能性がある。 
2027 年予算を巡る攻防は、単なる財政再建策の調整にとどまらない。大統領選挙を目前に控
え、各党が政権との距離の取り方を模索するなか、前年まで予算成立を支えた社会党との協力関
係は弱まり、代わってＲＮの対応が政権存続を左右する可能性が増している。ルコルニュ政権に
とって残された選択肢は、社会党かＲＮのどちらかに全面的に接近することではなく、双方との
交渉を続けながら、少なくとも一方が不信任に踏み切らないぎりぎりの妥協点を探ることにな
る。予算審議の行方は、大統領選挙を前にフランス政治の重心がどこへ移りつつあるのかを映し
出すことにもなりそうだ。

以 上

Date: 2026-10-16
Pages: 3

### Page 1

Meta 将成为 Ma7 中⾸个接受市场检验的 "AI 资本开⽀样本 " 。⾼盛预计，五⼤超⼤规模科技
公司明年合计资本⽀出将升⾄约 1.2 万亿美元，⽽半导体指数本⽉下跌 8% 、 CoreWeave 约
500 亿美元债务引发融资担忧，正令市场重新审视 AI 投资的可持续性。投资者将重点关注
Meta 能否证明 AI 投⼊正在转化为盈利增⻓，以及科技巨头的利润增速能否跑赢资本开⽀增
速，这将影响整个 AI 交易的后续⾛向。
摘要：
AI 交易动摇之际，市场将⽬光投向即将到来的财报季，寻找这场史⽆前例的科技投资浪潮能否兑
现回报的答案。
Meta 将率先在下周公布季度业绩，成为超⼤规模科技公司中⾸个接受市场检验的 "AI 资本开⽀样
本 " 。
⾼盛预测，包括 Meta 在内的五⼤超⼤规模公司明年合计资本⽀出将升⾄约 1.2 万亿美元，投资者
迫切希望从 Meta 财报中寻找这些巨额投⼊能否转化为可持续盈利的信号。与此同时，费城半导
体指数本⽉已累计下跌 8% ， AI 云计算公司 CoreWeave 因约 500 亿美元债务引发的融资⻛险警

报，令市场情绪愈发脆弱。
在此背景下， Meta 财报的意义已远超单⼀公司业绩本⾝ —— 它将为后续科技巨头财报定下基
调，并直接考验市场对 AI 投⼊产出⽐的定价逻辑是否站得住脚。
AI 交易出现裂痕，市场情绪趋于谨慎
⽀撑本轮⽜市⻓达四年的 AI 主线交易，近期出现明显动摇。半导体公司及戴尔科技等科技硬件企
业股价⼤幅回调，令市场信⼼承压。
据路透报道，德意志银⾏和加拿⼤皇家银⾏资本市场的策略师近期相继下调了对科技板块的推
荐评级。德意志银⾏策略师于 10 ⽉ 9 ⽇指出，科技板块的轮动⾏情 " 已⾛得很远，短期⻛险回报
⽐不再具有吸引⼒ " 。
Natixis Investment Managers Solutions 投资组合策略师 Garrett Melson 表⽰，" 市场叙事⽬前明
显有些不安， AI 现在处于何种位置存在很⼤的不确定性。 "他同时指出，这是⼀个 " ⾮此即彼 " 的
市场 ——" 当 AI 交易奏效时，它会把其他所有板块的氧⽓都抽⾛。 "
尽管科技和 AI 重仓股承压，标普 500 指数距本⽉创下的历史⾼点仍仅回落约 1% ，此前落后于科

技板块的其他⾏业正在填补空缺。
CoreWeave 债务危机敲响 AI 融资警钟
市场对 AI 建设热潮融资⻛险的担忧，因 CoreWeave 事件⽽骤然升温。这家 AI 云计算公司背负约
500 亿美元债务，其股价本周⼤幅下挫，引发投资者对 AI 基础设施融资可持续性的⼴泛质疑。
随着资本成本上升，超⼤规模科技公司的 " 资本⽀出狂潮 " 正变得愈发昂贵。 Horizon Investment
Services ⾸席执⾏官 Chuck Carlson 指出， " 资本成本上升意味着这场资本⽀出爆炸将变得更加昂
贵，这提⾼了回报的⻔槛。 Meta 将是第⼀个尝试就这⼀问题提供答案的公司。 "

与此同时，美国国债收益率上升及美联储进⼀步加息的预期也持续困扰股票投资者。本周偏热
的通胀数据强化了市场对美联储在 10 ⽉底会议上再度加息的押注，尽管期货定价显⽰多数投资
者仍预期美联储将等到 12 ⽉才会第⼆次加息。
Meta 财报：超⼤规模⽀出回报的⾸次⼤考
Meta 市值约 1.9 万亿美元，近期随着其 Muse AI 助⼿的推出，股价持续攀升。作为五⼤超⼤规模
科技公司之⼀， Meta 在 AI 数据中⼼和基础设施上的巨额投⼊，是推动整体企业盈利跃升的核⼼
驱动⼒之⼀。
根据 LSEG IBES 数据，标普 500 成分股公司第三季度盈利预计同⽐增⻓逾 30% ， AI 相关资本⽀出
是这⼀利润跳升的重要⽀撑。
BNY Wealth ⾼级股票分析师 Kevin Shea 表⽰， "本财报季最重要的看点之⼀，就是超⼤规模科

技公司的盈利能⼒能否以快于其资本⽀出增速的节奏增⻓。 "
⾼盛预测，五⼤超⼤规模公司明年合计资本⽀出将达约 1.2 万亿美元。在这⼀背景下， Meta 能否
展⽰出 AI 变现路径的清晰轮廓，将直接影响市场对整个 AI 投资周期可持续性的判断。
半导体及其他科技巨头财报同步受关注
芯⽚公司是本轮 AI 资本⽀出热潮的最⼤受益者之⼀，但相关股票近期已明显降温。费城半导体指
数本⽉累计下跌 8% ，尽管年内涨幅仍超过 60% ，远⾼于标普 500 指数同期约 13% 的涨幅。
投资者将于下周三关注 Lam Research 和 Texas Instruments 的财报，以获取半导体⾏业整体需求
的更⼴泛信号。两家公司股价今年均已上涨约 60% 。
此外，特斯拉、奈⻜、宝洁及 GE Aerospace 等⾼知名度公司的财报也将在下周陆续公布，共同
构成这⼀轮财报季的重要观察窗⼝。
UBS 全球财富管理分析师在周四的报告中指出， " ⼜⼀个强劲的财报季应有助于股市承受温和⾛
⾼的利率压⼒。 " 然⽽，在 AI 交易信⼼已出现裂痕的当下， Meta 能否率先交出⼀份令市场信服的

答卷，将在很⼤程度上决定这场万亿级 AI 豪赌的市场叙事能否延续。

Document 451: 习近平在内蒙古乌兰察布考察

Source PDF: all report pdf/习近平在内蒙古乌兰察布考察.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-16
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

习近平前往察哈尔⾼新技术开发区，在展⽰厅了解内蒙古⾃治区推进 “ 东数西算 ” ⼯程枢纽节
点建设和绿⾊算⼒产业发展等情况。他指出，实施 “ 东数西算 ” ⼯程、构建全国⼀体化算⼒
⽹，是党中央⼀项重要部署。要加强科学规划、要素⽀撑、安全保障，确保已建成项⽬⾼
效运⾏，并如期完成后续项⽬建设。要积极延伸产业链，推动数字经济与实体经济深度融
合，不断把算⼒优势转化为发展优势。
摘要：
中共中央总书记、国家主席、中央军委主席习近平 16 ⽇在内蒙古⾃治区乌兰察布市考察时强
调，绿⾊发展是⾼质量发展的应有之义，要统筹⾼质量发展和⾼⽔平保护，坚持⼭⽔林⽥湖草
沙⼀体化保护和系统治理，协同推进降碳、减污、扩绿、增⻓，坚定不移⾛⽣态优先、绿⾊发
展之路。
16 ⽇上午，习近平在内蒙古⾃治区党委书记王伟中和⾃治区⼈⺠政府主席包钢陪同下，来到乌
兰察布市考察调研。
位于乌兰察布市察哈尔右翼前旗的⻩旗海，是⼀个内陆封闭式湖泊，受⽓候等影响⼀度濒临⼲
涸。经过多年综合治理，⽔域⾯积逐步恢复扩⼤。习近平来到⻩旗海⾃然保护区，听取内蒙古

⾃治区加强⽣态环境保护和当地推进⻩旗海环境综合治理、⽣态系统修复等情况汇报，并实地
察看⽔岸滩涂⽣态、栖息⻦类等。他强调，湿地保护贵在保持原⽣态。要坚持尊重⾃然、顺应
⾃然、保护⾃然，全⾯实施湿地保护法，严守⽣态保护红线，着⼒提升⽣态系统质量和稳定
性，维护⽣物多样性。对退化的湿地⽣态系统，要坚持⾃然恢复为主、⼈⼯⼲预为辅，科学合
理加以修复。
乌兰察布⻛光资源丰沛，近些年发挥绿电、⽓候、区位等⽐较优势，绿⾊算⼒产业发展取得积
极成效。习近平前往察哈尔⾼新技术开发区，在展⽰厅了解内蒙古⾃治区推进 “ 东数西算 ” ⼯程
枢纽节点建设和绿⾊算⼒产业发展等情况。他指出，实施 “ 东数西算 ” ⼯程、构建全国⼀体化算
⼒⽹，是党中央⼀项重要部署。要加强科学规划、要素⽀撑、安全保障，确保已建成项⽬⾼效
运⾏，并如期完成后续项⽬建设。要积极延伸产业链，推动数字经济与实体经济深度融合，不
断把算⼒优势转化为发展优势。
随后，习近平同在现场的有关算⼒中⼼项⽬负责⼈、技术⼈员、察哈尔⾼新区⼯作⼈员等亲切

交流。他强调，算⼒⽹是国家规划建设的 “ 六张⽹ ” 之⼀，算⼒产业正处在加速发展阶段，前景
⼴阔。希望⼤家认真落实⾼效、绿⾊、安全的要求，注重 “ 算电协同 ” 、 “ 算⽔协同 ” ，搞好算⼒
中⼼项⽬建设和运营，积极推动绿⾊算⼒产业发展，为推进数字中国建设、促进新质⽣产⼒发
展多作贡献。习近平要求⾼新区加强党的领导和党的建设，优化各项公共服务和要素保障，为
企业和各类⼈才⼲事创业营造良好环境。
习近平还察看了当地特⾊农牧产品展⽰，详细了解主要品种和品质特点，⿎励当地加强产销衔
接，促进农牧⺠增收致富。
考察途中，习近平强调，我国幅员辽阔，东西南北中资源禀赋差异明显。新征程上，各地区要
按照党中央战略决策和规划部署，树⽴和践⾏正确政绩观，完整准确全⾯贯彻新发展理念，紧
跟时代潮流、抢抓发展机遇，⽴⾜⾃⾝条件、发挥⽐较优势，因地制宜推动⾼质量发展。
何⽴峰及中央和国家机关有关部⻔负责同志陪同考察。
本⽂来源：央视新闻

Document 483: 匈牙利国别研究专题报告：扎根匈牙利制造业，链接欧洲统一市场

Source PDF: all report pdf/匈牙利国别研究专题报告：扎根匈牙利制造业，链接欧洲统一市场.pdf
Report source: 国元证券
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 73
Status: ok

### Page 1

请务必阅读正文之后的免责条款部分 
  1/73

[Table_Main]

证券研究报告 
国别研究|匈牙利 2026 年10 月 13 日 
[Table_Title] 扎根匈牙利制造业，链接欧洲统一市场 
——匈牙利国别研究专题报告

[Table_Summary] 报告要点： 
 外资链主深耕三十余年，欧盟准入与出口制造塑造中东欧门户 
匈牙利的产业基础建立在外资链主、欧盟市场准入和出口制造能力之
上。20 世纪 90 年代，铃木与奥迪先后布局整车、发动机及配套环节；
2004 年加入欧盟后，德国整车企业、亚洲电池企业与本地供应链继续
集聚。经过三十余年发展，匈牙利当前已形成以外资链主为核心、面
向欧洲市场的出口制造网络。 2025 年，匈牙利制造业增加值占 GDP 比
重为 15.1% ， 货物和服务出口相当于 GDP 的 72.6% ， 欧盟市场约占其出
口的四分之三， 德国占其出口的 25.3% 。 从招商引资成效看， 匈牙利投
资促进局（ HIPA ）2025 年支持 108 个项目，投资额 70.69 亿欧元，预
计创造 18,227 个岗位；其中中国投资 39.7 亿欧元，连续三年居各来
源国首位。 对中资企业而言， 匈牙利更适合作为欧洲生产和交付节点：
先承接规模制造，再借助欧盟单一市场、交通走廊和成熟客户体系覆
盖欧洲。  
 汽车和电池仍是投资主线，电子制造与专业服务提供补充增长 
汽车与动力电池仍是匈牙利承接产业转移的主线，产业空间由西北部
传统整车走廊向东部电池集群和南部新能源整车基地延伸。奥迪、梅
赛德斯-奔驰、铃木、宝马等整车企业形成存量制造基础，比亚迪、宁
德时代及电池材料企业带来新增投资，整车、电池、电子和零部件之
间的客户半径随之缩短。汽车之外，布达佩斯及区域城市已形成电子
制造、商务服务中心、生命科学、化工新材料、食品加工和新能源等
产业基础：电子与共享服务承接跨国公司区域职能，医药依托研发、
临床试验和生产体系，化工依托万华化学宝思德基地向高附加值材料
延伸，农业食品则连接本地原料与欧盟消费市场。多元产业结构使匈
牙利既能承接大型制造项目，也为设备、检测、物流、数字化和专业
服务企业提供跟随空间。  
 税收补贴降低前期成本，审批与要素条件影响项目落地 
匈牙利通过低税率、税收抵免和现金补贴降低项目成本。当前企业所
得税率为 9%，低于波兰 19% 、捷克 21% 和罗马尼亚 16% 的一般税率。符
合条件的投资可申请发展性税收优惠，当年抵免最高可达应纳企业所
得税额的 80% ；达到地区投资门槛的项目还可申请 VIP 现金补贴，最
低投资额按地区分为 200 万、300 万、500 万或 1000 万欧元，补贴随
建设进度和收入、工资、研发等履约结果拨付。税率优势并不等于全
部税负较低。 地方营业税最高为 2%；27% 的标准增值税虽然可依法抵扣
或退还， 但缴税与退税的时间差会占用建设期现金； 合并收入达到 7.5
亿欧元的大型跨国集团还需纳入 15% 全球最低税框架。大型制造项目
还要核验投资审 查、电网接入、供排水、环境容量、土地交付和外籍
人员许可。项目回报最终取决于税费、补贴兑现、审批周期和生产要
素保障，需要在选址和交易设计阶段统一测算。总体来看，匈牙利能
够降低制造业项目的初始投入，但企业仍需把名义税率、补贴兑现和
建设运营条件纳入同一套项目模型，避免低估实际落地成本。

[Table_Summary]  五类进入路径多线并举，中资布局由制造落地向区域经营延伸 
绿地建厂由企业新设生产基地并自行组织设备、人员和供应链，适合
订单明确且希望掌控制造质量的项目，代表企业包括比亚迪和联想集
团。并购整合通过收购当地企业或资产取得客户、团队和现成产能，
能够缩短进入周期，但需承担历史责任和整合成本，代表企业包括万
华化学和均胜电子。客户跟随围绕欧洲核心客户就近建设配套产能，
前置订单降低初期需求风险，但对客户排产的依赖较高，代表企业包
括宁德时代和亿纬锂能。区域功能平台从销售、仓储、售后或设备制
造切入，再随欧洲业务增长增加研发和区域管理职能，代表企业包括
华为和蔚来汽车。合资及项目合作通 过项目公司和长期合同整合当地
资源，适合铁路、能源等建设周期较长的领域，代表项目包括中国中
铁参与的匈塞铁路和中机公司建设的光伏电站。五类路径的差异主要
在于客户基础、资本投入和本地资源依赖程度。随着在匈业务深化，
中资布局正由单一制造节点逐步延伸至服务、研发和区域运营，企业
需要根据欧洲订单、审批条件和自身组织能力选择进入方式。

投资建议： 
机构层面， 匈牙利兼具欧盟市场通道、中东欧制造基础和较好的对华
合作条件，是中资企业布局欧洲的重要节点。金融机构可围绕汽车及
汽车电子、新能源、电子信息和化工等产业，结合企业建厂、 供应链
及区域经营需求提供跨境金融服务，并重点考察欧洲市场需求、政策
环境、项目进度和本地经营能力。  
企业层面， 匈牙利的区位、税收和制造业配套有助于企业连接欧盟市
场，但用工、能源和审批条件也会影响项目推进。企业可根据客户基
础、产业链位置和欧洲业务目标，选择绿地建厂、客户跟随、并购整
合、区域功能平台或项目合作等进入路径。当前中资布局正由制造向
销售、服务和研发延伸，经营成效主要取决于欧洲订单、本地配套和
组织能力。  
政府层面， 中资企业在匈布局正由制造项目向供应链配套和区域经营
延伸，公共服务需求也更加综合。国内有关部门可围绕产业信息、政
策解读、项目对接和风险服务，加强与驻外机构、商协会、金融机构
及当地园区的联系，持续跟踪欧盟规则、投资审查和生产要素变化，
帮助企业完善国别认知和市场判断，为进入时点、合作方向和区域布
局提供参考。

风险提示 
投资与贸易审查趋严风险； 补贴拨付延迟与履约条件变化风险； 欧洲需求走
弱与客户排产不足风险； 许可、 公用工程和环境容量不匹配风险； 劳动力收
缩与外籍人员政策变化风险； 税务口径偏差与补贴追缴风险； 福林汇率与融
资成本波动风险；投后整合、客户合同和数据合规风险。

1. 制造业立国、外资驱动，开放型经济依托欧洲市场 
1.1 从市场化转轨到融入欧盟，外向型经济格局逐步成形 
匈牙利是典型的小型开放经济， 国内市场规模有限， 制造业、 外资和欧洲市场共同构
成其经济运行的基本框架。匈牙利地处中欧内陆， 国土面积约9.3 万平方公里，2025
年人口约 950 万人，首都布达佩斯兼具全国消费中心、交通枢纽和区域服务中心功
能。匈牙利于2004 年加入欧盟、2007 年加入申根区，目前仍使用福林，尚未加入欧
元区。 这一制度组合使企业可以在欧盟单一市场内组织生产和销售， 同时仍需承担福
林兑欧元波动带来的成本与融资影响。2025 年，匈牙利制造业增加值占国内生产总
值的15.1%，货物和服务出口相当于国内生产总值的72.6%，欧盟约占其出口的四分
之三。 由此看， 匈牙利经济对本地消费的依赖相对较低， 制造业项目通常以欧洲客户、
跨境供应链和出口订单为需求基础。 
当前由外资链主带动的工业结构，形成于 20 世纪 90 年代由计划经济向市场经济转
轨的过程。1989 年以后，匈牙利把企业改制、私有化和吸收外资作为经济重组的重
要抓手。 经济合作与发展组织 （OECD） 的回顾显示， 截至1999 年 11 月， 原私有化机
构所管理企业中超过90%已完成私有化， 私人部门占国内生产总值的比重升至约85%；
1990 年至 1999 年 11 月，外资通过私有化进入匈牙利的规模达到 126 亿美元，并贡
献了约三分之二的私有化收入。 外资由此较快进入银行、 制药、 化工、 食品、 公用事
业和制造业。铃木于 1991 年在埃斯泰尔戈姆投资建厂，奥迪于 1993 年在杰尔设立
生产基地， 汽车、 电子等行业随后围绕跨国公司形成出口制造和专业化配套。 匈牙利
在转轨初期直接借助外资补充资金、 技术、 管理和海外订单， 工业增长因此较早嵌入
欧洲生产网络，也形成了对跨国公司投资节奏和外部需求较为敏感的结构特征。 
加入欧盟以后，匈牙利开放型经济的制度基础进一步稳定，外资来源也由西欧逐步
扩展至亚洲。欧盟成员国身份把关税同盟、 产品规则、人员流动和区域资金纳入统一
框架，申根区身份则降低了跨境人员和物流组织成本。2008 年国际金融危机及随后
欧洲需求放缓， 使匈牙利重新强调制造业和出口。2010 年以后， 政府推进再工业化，
并通过“向东开放”政策加强与中国、 韩国、 日本等亚洲经济体的贸易和投资联系。
2020 年以来，欧洲汽车产业向电动化转型，匈牙利原有整车和零部件基础开始与韩
国、 中国的动力电池、 新能源汽车及电子项目衔接， 外资结构由德系整车主导逐步转
向欧洲整车企业与亚洲电动化企业共同扩张。与此同时，2022 年以来的能源价格波
动、 德国制造业需求偏弱和高通胀再次暴露出进口能源、 外部订单和汇率变化对匈牙
利经济的影响。 梳理上述脉络可以看到， 匈牙利当前的产业条件经过三轮重组：20 世
纪90 年代市场化转轨引入外资， 加入欧盟后接入统一市场，2010 年以来再工业化和
亚洲资本进入推动产业向电动化延伸。 后续判断区位、 产业和政策优势时， 需要同时
观察欧洲市场需求、跨国公司投资以及本地要素承载能力。

### Page 7

请务必阅读正文之后的免责条款部分                                                                       7/73

1.2 欧盟腹地与中东欧交通节点，构成“在匈生产、面向欧洲”的区位基
础 
匈牙利连接欧盟市场、组织跨境供应链的区位作用较为突出，但本地市场对规模化
制造的支撑相对有限。匈牙利地处中欧腹地， 自2004 年加入欧盟、2007 年加入申根
区以来， 货物、 资本与商业人员可在欧盟单一市场和申根框架内自由流动， 制度层面
已深度融入欧洲经济体系。从市场纵深看，以匈牙利为圆心、半径1000 公里的范围
内覆盖约2 亿消费者；从通道条件看，五条泛欧交通网（TEN-T）走廊穿境而过，境
内高速公路通车里程约1871 公里，陆路向西衔接德国—奥地利工业带，向南经巴尔
干半岛通达亚得里亚海港口与希腊方向， 向东连接乌克兰、 罗马尼亚并指向黑海， 形
成欧洲中部“东西—南北”十字交汇的运输组织节点。对制造类和区域分拨类项目
而言， 这一区位意味着“在匈生产、 面向欧洲”： 在关税同盟与申根框架内组织跨境
供应链， 德国、 意大利、 波兰等主要市场大多处于公路一至两日运输半径之内。 与之
相对，匈牙利本国人口 2025 年为 951.4 万人，且自 2015 年以来缓慢下降，本地终
端消费足以支撑区域销售总部和售后服务网络，但不足以单独消化规模化制造产能，
投资测算的需求基数应以欧盟及中东欧辐射市场为准，本地市场仅作次要变量。 
具体而言， 匈牙利的门户功能可分为三类。 其一， 面向欧盟的本地生产与原产地安排，
产品在匈牙利完成实质性加工、满足欧盟原产地及海关规则并取得欧盟货物地位后，
可在欧盟关税同盟内部流通， 不再征收成员国间关税。 这是电动汽车、 动力电池等贸
易救济敏感行业在匈布局的重要动因。 其二， 面向中东欧的仓储、 售后与供应链服务，
匈牙利位于德语区、斯拉夫语区和巴尔干地区交汇处，商务服务中心（ Business 
Service Center， 以下简称“BSC”） 行业可用40 余种语言提供服务， 适合承接区域
分拨、 维修、 客服和供应链管理职能。 其三， 面向德系整车及欧洲工业客户的就近配
套， 杰尔、 布达佩斯周边及中部地区已形成较成熟的汽车零部件短距离供货体系。 匈
牙利并非所有产品进入欧洲的低成本通道： 项目仍受欧盟贸易救济、 外国补贴审查、
CE 等产品认证以及本地价值要求约束。原产地资格与关税优惠仍需逐产品、逐工艺
认定，仅凭“欧盟境内生产”不足以直接得出结论。 
匈牙利同时具备欧盟成员国、申根国家和中欧交通节点三项条件。布达佩斯1000 公
里范围内可覆盖约 2 亿消费者，五条泛欧交通网走廊穿境而过；产业方面，德系整
车、 中韩电池企业和布达佩斯商务服务中心已经形成较为完整的制造与服务网络。 与
波兰、捷克、斯洛伐克、罗马尼亚、塞尔维亚相比（表2），匈牙利的差异化优势在
于：欧盟最低的 9%企业所得税、五走廊交汇的地理中心物流区位，以及整车与电池
链主集聚带来的配套密度——波兰胜在本地市场规模， 捷克、 斯洛伐克强于制造传统，
罗马尼亚依托IT 服务与低成本，塞尔维亚尚在欧盟门槛之外，匈牙利则是同时承接
“面向欧盟组织生产”与“亚洲电动化产能转移”的双向节点。

1.3 从德系制造到亚洲电动化投资，外资持续重塑匈牙利工业结构 
匈牙利形成了以外资链主、出口制造和本地专业化配套为核心的工业体系，其优势
在于承接跨国生产网络，约束则在于关键技术、外部需求和进口能源依赖较高。 20
世纪90 年代转轨时期，铃木落子埃斯泰尔戈姆，奥迪布局杰尔，奠定整车与发动机
制造基础。2004 年加入欧盟后，德系整车与零部件企业加速集聚，奔驰在凯奇凯梅
特建立整车厂，电子、零部件和工程服务随之成型。2010 年前后，匈牙利提出“向
东开放”政策，主动对接亚洲产业资本。2020 年以来，电动化浪潮促使外资结构进
一步多元化： 宝马德布勒森工厂于2025 年进入投产阶段， 三星SDI、SK On 等韩国电

### Page 10

请务必阅读正文之后的免责条款部分                                                                       10/73

池企业扩产， 宁德时代、 亿纬锂能、 欣旺达及比亚迪等中国企业相继布局， 产业主导
力量由德系整车延伸至中韩电池链主。2025 年，匈牙利吸收中国对欧盟及英国直接
投资的23%。从欧洲范围的行业结构看，同年中国对欧洲汽车行业投资的93%投向电
动汽车供应链， 电动化仍是中资制造业赴欧投资的重要方向。 同年， 匈牙利投资促进
局（HIPA）支持的108 个项目涉及计划投资额70.69 亿欧元、计划新增18,227 个就
业岗位； 电子、 汽车和化工行业对应投资额分别为38.8 亿、11.0 亿和8.19 亿欧元。 
外资项目扩大了匈牙利的制造和出口能力，也加深了其对外部需求、进口能源和跨
国公司决策的依赖。一方面，制造产能、 出口、 就业与技术工种快速集聚： 匈牙利制
造业增加值占GDP 比重2025 年仍达15.1%。图4 显示，该比重较2015 年的约20.0%
累计回落约5 个百分点，2024—2025 年降速有所加快： 一是欧盟整车市场景气下行、
德国等核心需求疲弱，传统整车与零部件环节率先承压；二是2022—2023 年进口能
源价格高企推高制造成本， 能耗密集环节收缩； 三是欧元区高利率与福林波动令设备
投资与产线扩张普遍推迟；四是商务服务、ICT 等服务业扩张快于制造业，GDP 分母
结构变化亦压低制造业份额；而宝马德布勒森新工厂于2025 年投产，宁德时代和比
亚迪等在建项目仍处于建设或投产准备阶段， 相关增量尚未充分计入， 份额下行更多
反映了传统环节收缩与新产能爬坡之间的“青黄不接”， 制造业内部结构正由“传统
整车与零部件”向“电动化与电池链”切换。2025 年匈牙利货物和服务出口相当于
GDP 的 72.6%。较低的企业所得税率以及整车、零部件和商务服务机构的集中布局，
缩短了新项目寻找供应商、 招聘人员和响应客户的时间。这一优势落到具体项目上更
为直观： 链主周边已集聚较多一级供应商和专业服务机构， 可缩短部分配套环节的响
应距离，约 70 家重点 Tier 1 供应商与 245 家商务服务中心构成可即时触达的供应
商池和多语言人才池， 这类产业集聚通常有助于改善供应商寻源、 产线爬坡和工程师
招聘条件； 对供应链出海的配套企业而言， 客户跟随型项目通常以既有定点或明确合
作关系为前提， 链主邻近布局可缩短交付距离。另一方面， 短板同样明显： 本土增加
值率有限， 高价值环节的研发决策与核心知识产权多由跨国公司母国总部掌控， 本地
供应商多集中于二、 三级配套， 深度不足； 宏观层面则表现为对德国及欧盟外部需求、
进口能源和福林汇率的高敏感性，2022—2023 年能源与通胀冲击将其拖入技术性衰
退。 劳动力收缩、 工资上涨和补贴复核会继续抬高项目成本。 对面向欧洲市场的制造
项目而言，提高自动化水平有助于缓解用工约束， 但出口需求仍决定产能利用率。 人
口收缩和能源依赖短期内难以改变， 项目只能通过选址、 自动化和工艺调整降低影响。
补贴复核会增加前期不确定性， 但新项目若能明确研发、 就业和本地采购贡献， 仍有
机会获得支持。 本地配套则取决于链主投产节奏和采购比例，2025 年HIPA 支持项目
中超过84%落在首都以外。

1.4 小型开放经济处于弱增长阶段，产业机会与宏观约束并存 
匈牙利工业基础未因短期弱增长而消失， 但低投资、 较高通胀、 财政压力、 德国需求
疲弱和福林波动会直接改变出海项目的销量、融资和成本假设。2018—2019 年匈牙
利实际GDP 增速曾达5%上下，2022—2023 年受进口能源冲击与高通胀拖累，2023 年
GDP 下降0.8%、CPI 同比高达17.1%；2024 年GDP 增长0.7%、2025 年GDP 增长0.4%，
仍处弱修复区间，2025 年通胀为 4.4%、失业率约 4.4%。进入 2026 年，增长动能有
所改善：据匈牙利中央统计局第二次估算，2026 年二季度GDP 同比增长1.7%，经季
节和日历因素调整后同比同样增长1.7%、 环比增长0.5%， 上半年累计增长1.7%，服
务业与工业是主要正向贡献。欧盟委员会2026 年春季预测匈牙利2026 年、2027 年
分别增长 1.8%和 2.1%，通胀回落至 3.2%和 3.1%；但财政端约束不容忽视——财政
赤字率预计由 2025 年的 4.7%升至 2026 年的 6.2%、2027 年仍有 5.8%，政府债务率
0
